\displaystyle=-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}{W}^{\kappa}+{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta})+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}

\displaystyle={W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\mathrm{vort}^{\alpha}\mathbf{u})-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}{W}^{\kappa}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}-\mathrm{e}^{-h}w^{\alpha}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}{W}^{\kappa}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}.

(2.38)

On the other hand, we also have

\begin{split}\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}=&\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}(\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}w_{\kappa})+\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}w_{\kappa}\end{split}

(2.39)

Inserting (
2.4
) to (
2.39
), it yields

\begin{split}\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w^{\alpha}=&\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}(\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}-u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\alpha}+u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w_{\gamma}\\
&\ +u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma})+\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}w_{\kappa}\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}+\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}(-u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\alpha}+u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w_{\gamma}+u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}\\
&\ -u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma})+\partial^{\alpha}(\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})+\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{\kappa}{W}^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}h-\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}{W}^{\kappa}.\end{split}

(2.40)

Seeing from (
1.18
), we can derive that

\begin{split}\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}=&\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}u^{\gamma}(W^{\delta}-c_{s}^{-2}\epsilon^{\delta\beta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu})\\
=&-\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}\epsilon^{\delta\beta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u^{\gamma}u_{\beta}w_{\nu}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h.\end{split}

(2.41)

Combing (
2.3
), (
2.41
), and (
2.40
), we get

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{1}=&-\epsilon_{\kappa\ \gamma\delta}^{\ \alpha}\epsilon^{\delta\beta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u^{\gamma}u_{\beta}w_{\nu}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h+\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}(-u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\alpha}+u_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w_{\gamma})\\
&+\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}(u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-u^{\alpha}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}w_{\gamma})+\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{\kappa}{W}^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}h-\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}{W}^{\kappa}\\
&+\partial^{\alpha}(\mathrm{e}^{-h}{W}^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}{W}^{\kappa}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}.\end{split}

(2.42)

As for
\mathrm{R}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{2}=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u_{\delta}{W}^{\kappa}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}{W}^{\kappa}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}{W}^{\kappa}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa}

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}u_{\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}u_{\delta}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\delta}){W}^{\kappa}(u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{e}^{-h}w^{\alpha}{W}^{\kappa}(-\partial_{\kappa}h-u_{\kappa}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{e}^{-h}w^{\alpha}{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h.

(2.43)

For
\mathrm{R}_{3}
, we deduce that

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{3}=&2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta\eta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\big(u^{\eta}\partial^{\mu}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\nu}u_{\kappa}\big)\\
=&-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\big(u^{\eta}\partial^{\mu}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\nu}u_{\kappa}\big)\left(\delta^{\alpha}_{\eta}\delta^{\gamma}_{\mu}\delta^{\beta}_{\nu}-\delta^{\alpha}_{\eta}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}\delta^{\gamma}_{\nu}+\delta^{\beta}_{\eta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\gamma}_{\nu}-\delta^{\beta}_{\eta}\delta^{\gamma}_{\mu}\delta^{\alpha}_{\nu}+\delta^{\gamma}_{\eta}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}\delta^{\alpha}_{\nu}-\delta^{\gamma}_{\eta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\beta}_{\nu}\right)\\
=&-2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{31}}+2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{32}}-2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{33}}\\
&+2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{34}}-2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{35}}+2\underbrace{u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa})}_{\equiv\mathrm{R}_{36}}.\end{split}

So we have

\mathrm{R}_{3}=-2\mathrm{R}_{31}+2\mathrm{R}_{32}-2\mathrm{R}_{33}+2\mathrm{R}_{34}-2\mathrm{R}_{35}+2\mathrm{R}_{36}.

(2.44)

Let us now calculate
\mathrm{R}_{31},\mathrm{R}_{32},\cdots
, and
\mathrm{R}_{36}
. For
\mathrm{R}_{31}
, we firstly have

\mathrm{R}_{31}=u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}).

(2.45)

Secondly, we can also get

\displaystyle u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa})=

\displaystyle u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\big(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\big)-u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}-u^{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\big(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\big)-u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}-u^{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}).

(2.46)

Substituting (
2.3
) to (
2.45
), it follows

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{31}=&u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\big(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\big)-u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}\\
&+u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}-u^{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}).\end{split}

(2.47)

For
\mathrm{R}_{32}
, we can compute it by

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{32}=&u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+u^{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}).\end{split}

(2.48)

Using (
2.17
), we can rewrite (
2.48
) as

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{32}=&u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa})-u^{\alpha}u^{\kappa}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})\\
&+u^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})-u^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}).\end{split}

(2.49)

For
\mathrm{R}_{33}
is the most difficult term, we postpone to consider it. We turn to calculate
\mathrm{R}_{34}
now. Using the chain rule,
u_{\beta}u^{\beta}=-1
, and
u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\beta}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{34}=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}-\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}).

(2.50)

On the other hand, we can rewrite

-\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})=\partial^{\alpha}(-\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})+\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}).

(2.51)

Inserting (
2.51
) to (
2.3
), we obtain

\mathrm{R}_{34}=\partial^{\alpha}(-\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa})-\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}+\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}.

(2.52)

For
\mathrm{R}_{35}
, by the chain rule and (
2.17
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{35}=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})-u^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle+u^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})-u^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}).

(2.53)

For
\mathrm{R}_{36}
, a direct calculation tells us

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{36}=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}+u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}).

(2.54)

Note

\displaystyle u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}u_{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa})=

\displaystyle u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta})-u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}-u^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\kappa}w^{\beta}).

(2.55)

Combining (
2.3
) with (
2.3
), we therefore have

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{36}=&u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta})-u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}+u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}\\
&-u^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\kappa}w^{\beta})+u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}.\end{split}

(2.56)

It still remains for us to calculate
\mathrm{R}_{33}
. Using the chain rule,
u_{\beta}u^{\beta}=-1
, and
u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\beta}=0
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{33}=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(u^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+u_{\beta}u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})+u_{\beta}u^{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}-\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\lambda}.

(2.57)

By using (
2.20
) and
u_{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\beta}=0
, we can compute out

\displaystyle-\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\big\{\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}(\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{u})+\partial_{\lambda}(\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})-u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})

\displaystyle+2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial^{\beta}u^{\gamma}-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}u^{\beta}+u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\beta}\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\big\{\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}(\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{u})+\partial_{\lambda}(\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\}+u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\big\{\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\big\}

\displaystyle-(2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial^{\beta}u^{\gamma}-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}u^{\beta}+u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\beta})\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}

\displaystyle-u^{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}+u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}.

(2.58)

By using (
1.14
), we have

\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\{\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}(\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{u})\}=\partial^{\alpha}(w^{\lambda}\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}\mathbf{w})-w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}\mathbf{w}).

(2.59)

Using (
1.13
), we also derive

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\{\partial_{\lambda}(\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\}=

\displaystyle-\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}(c^{-2}_{s}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\big\{-c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\lambda}hu^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-c_{s}^{-2}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\partial_{\lambda}h)\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}

\displaystyle-u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})+c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}hu^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})+u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(c_{s}^{-2})\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}+3c_{s}^{-1}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}

\displaystyle+u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(-c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})+c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}hu^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}).

(2.60)

Combining (
2.3
), (
2.3
), (
2.59
), and (
2.3
), we obtain

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{33}=&u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\left\{\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}+c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h)\right\}-\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\\
&-(2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial^{\beta}u^{\gamma}-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}u^{\beta}+u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\beta})\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\\
&+c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-3c_{s}^{-1}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-c_{s}^{-2}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})\\
&-\partial^{\alpha}(w^{\lambda}\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}\mathbf{w})+w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}\mathbf{w})-u^{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}).\end{split}

(2.61)

Combining (
2.44
), (
2.47
), (
2.49
), (
2.61
), (
2.52
), (
2.3
) and (
2.56
), we have

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{3}=&\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma+u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\left(-2u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}+2u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\right)+2u^{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\\
&-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}-2u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}-2u^{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\beta}(\partial^{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\\
&+2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}+2u_{\beta}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa})-2u^{\alpha}u^{\kappa}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})\\
&+2u^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})-2u^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa})+4\partial^{\gamma}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\\
&+(4u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial^{\beta}u^{\gamma}-4u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}u_{\lambda}-2u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}u^{\beta}+2u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\beta})\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\\
&-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}+6c_{s}^{-1}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}+2c_{s}^{-2}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})\\
&+2\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\mathrm{vort}_{\lambda}\mathbf{w}+2u^{\kappa}(u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\kappa}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})-2\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\\
&-2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}-2u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})+2u^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})\\
&-2u^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})+2u^{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}u^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}u_{\kappa})-2u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\beta}\\
&+2u_{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa})\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}-2u^{\kappa}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{\kappa}w^{\beta})+2u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial^{\beta}u_{\kappa}.\end{split}

(2.62)

It remains for us to consider
\mathrm{R}_{4},\mathrm{R}_{5},\mathrm{R}_{6},\mathrm{R}_{7}
and
\mathrm{R}_{8}
. They don’t have derivatives loss given energy estimates, so it’s enough for us to use chain rules. For
\mathrm{R}_{4}
, we have

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{4}=&-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\mu}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\mu}u^{\kappa}\\
&+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}h.\end{split}

(2.63)

Similarly, we can compute out

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{5}=&-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\partial_{\gamma}h+2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\delta}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-h}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}h,\end{split}

(2.64)

and

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{6}=&\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(c_{s}^{-2}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta})\\
=&-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\eta}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\\
&+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\eta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\mu}h\\
&+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon^{\kappa\eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\delta},\end{split}

(2.65)

and

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{7}=&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}(c_{s}^{-2}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})\\
=&2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\\
&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\mu}h\\
&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w_{\nu}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa},\end{split}

(2.66)

and

\begin{split}\mathrm{R}_{8}=&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\{(c^{-2}_{s}+2)u_{\eta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h\}\\
=&2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}(c^{-2}_{s}+2)u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\eta}\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h\\
&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}(c^{-2}_{s}+2)u_{\eta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}(c^{-2}_{s}+2)u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\mu}h\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\\
&-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}(c^{-2}_{s}+2)u_{\beta}u_{\eta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\nu}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\mu}h.\end{split}

(2.67)

Substituting (
2.37
), (
2.42
), (
2.3
), (
2.62
), (
2.63
), (
2.64
), (
2.65
), (
2.66
), and (
2.67
) to (
2.34
), we therefore get (
2.28
).
∎

We turn to introduce a good wave formulation for
\mathbf{u}_{+}
as follows.

Lemma 2.18
.

Let
\mathbf{u}_{+}
be defined in (
1.26
). Then
\mathbf{u}_{+}
satisfies the following wave equation

\begin{split}\square_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{u}_{+}=&\mathbf{g}^{0\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\mathbf{Z}+\mathbf{Q}+\mathbf{B},\end{split}

(2.68)

where
\mathbf{Q}=(Q^{0},Q^{1},Q^{2},Q^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
has defined in (
1.22
),
\mathbf{Z}=(Z^{0},Z^{1},Z^{2},Z^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
and
\mathbf{B}=(B^{0},B^{1},B^{2},B^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
are given by

\begin{split}\mathbf{Z}=&-\Omega(u^{0})^{2}(c_{s}^{2}+1)\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-},\end{split}

(2.69)

and

\begin{split}\mathbf{B}=&-((u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}+\mathbf{g}^{0i})\partial_{i}\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{T}u^{0}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}\\
&\ -u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}.\end{split}

(2.70)

Proof.

For
\mathbf{u}=\mathbf{u}_{+}+\mathbf{u}_{-}
, so we have

\square_{g}u^{\alpha}=\square_{g}u_{+}^{\alpha}+\square_{g}u_{-}^{\alpha}.

(2.71)

On the other hand, by (
1.20
) and (
1.24
), we know

\begin{split}&\square_{g}u^{\alpha}=-\Omega c_{s}^{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}W^{\alpha}+Q^{\alpha},\end{split}

(2.72)

and

\begin{split}&(\mathrm{I}+\mathrm{P}^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma})u_{-}^{\alpha}=-\mathrm{e}^{-h}W^{\alpha}.\end{split}

(2.73)

Due to (
2.71
) and (
2.72
), it yields

\square_{g}u_{+}^{\alpha}+\square_{g}u_{-}^{\alpha}=\Omega c^{2}_{s}(\mathrm{I}+\mathrm{P}^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma})u_{-}^{\alpha}+Q^{\alpha}.

(2.74)

By using (
1.24
) again, we have

\mathrm{P}^{\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta\gamma}^{2}u^{\alpha}_{-}=m^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}.

(2.75)

Inserting (
2.75
) to (
2.74
), we infer

\square_{g}u_{+}^{\alpha}+\square_{g}u_{-}^{\alpha}=\Omega c^{2}_{s}\left(m^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}\right)+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}+Q^{\alpha}.

(2.76)

Also, from the expression of
g
, we have

\square_{g}u_{-}^{\alpha}=\Omega c_{s}^{2}m^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u_{-}^{\alpha}+\Omega(c_{s}^{2}-1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u_{-}^{\alpha},

(2.77)

Substituting (
2.77
) to (
2.76
), one can see

\square_{g}u^{\alpha}_{+}=\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}+{Q}^{\alpha}.

(2.78)

Referring Lemma
2.13
, (
2.78
) is not so good for us to use this modified Duhamel’s principle. So we need to rewrite (
2.78
) in another way. Write (
2.78
) as

\begin{split}\square_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{u}_{+}=&\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}\\
=&\mathbf{g}^{00}(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}.\end{split}

(2.79)

A simple calculation tells us

\begin{split}&(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\\
=&u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}-u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\\
=&\mathbf{T}\{u^{0}(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}-\mathbf{T}u^{0}\{(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}\\
&-u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}.\end{split}

(2.80)

Substituting (
2.80
) to (
2.79
), we have

\begin{split}\square_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{u}_{+}=&\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\beta}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\beta\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}\\
=&\mathbf{g}^{00}\mathbf{T}\{u^{0}(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}\\
&-\mathbf{T}u^{0}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}-u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}.\end{split}

(2.81)

Consider
\mathbf{g}^{00}=-1
. For any function
F
, we can also derive that

\mathbf{g}^{0\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}F=-\partial_{t}F+\mathbf{g}^{0i}\partial_{i}F=\mathbf{g}^{00}\mathbf{T}F-\left((u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}+\mathbf{g}^{0i}\right)\partial_{i}F.

(2.82)

Seeing from (
2.82
) and (
2.81
), so we have

\begin{split}\square_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{u}_{+}=&\mathbf{g}^{0\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\{u^{0}(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}\\
&-\left((u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}+\mathbf{g}^{0i}\right)\partial_{i}\{u^{0}(\mathbf{g}^{00})^{-1}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}\\
&-\mathbf{T}u^{0}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}-u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\\
=&\mathbf{g}^{0\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\{-(u^{0})^{2}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}+\mathbf{Q}+\Omega c^{2}_{s}u_{-}^{\alpha}\\
&+\left((u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}+\mathbf{g}^{0i}\right)\partial_{i}\{u^{0}\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}\\
&-\mathbf{T}u^{0}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}\}-u^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}\{\Omega(c_{s}^{2}+1)u^{\gamma}\}\partial_{\gamma}\mathbf{u}_{-}.\end{split}

Noting (
2.69
) and (
2.70
), so we have proved (
2.68
).
∎

Remark 2.4
.

By (
2.68
), thus
\mathbf{u}_{+}
satisfies a better wave equation than
\mathbf{u}
. Moreover, (
2.68
) plays a key role in Strichartz estimates for
\mathbf{u}_{+}
.

2.4.
Elliptic estimates

In this subsection, we mainly prove some useful elliptic estimates. We first derive introduce a space-time elliptic estimate.

2.4.1.
Space-time elliptic estimate

Lemma 2.19
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{u})
be a solution of (
1.20
). Let
\mathbf{u}_{-}
,
\mathbf{P}
, and
\mathbf{T}
be defined in (
1.24
), (
1.26
), (
1.32
). Then
\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-}
satisfies

\begin{split}\mathbf{P}(\mathbf{T}u^{\alpha}_{-})=&(u^{0})^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-h}c^{2}_{s}W^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(u^{0})^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-h}\big\{{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}-{2}{W}^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u^{\alpha}{W}^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\\
&-{2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}w^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\big\}\\
&-(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}+(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}_{-}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(\frac{u^{\kappa}}{u^{0}})\\
&+(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial^{2}_{\beta\kappa}u^{\alpha}_{-}+(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial^{2}_{\kappa\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}.\end{split}

(2.83)

Moreover, the following estimate holds:

\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}(\|(u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}},h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H_{x}^{s}}+\|(u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}},h)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H_{x}^{s}}).

(2.84)

Proof.

Recall
\mathbf{T}=(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}
in (
1.32
). Operating
\mathbf{T}
on (
1.26
), we get

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathbf{P}u^{\alpha}_{-})=&\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-h}W^{\alpha})=-\mathrm{e}^{-h}W^{\alpha}\mathbf{T}h+\mathrm{e}^{-h}\mathbf{T}W^{\alpha}.\end{split}

By using (
1.13
) and (
2.15
), we have

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathbf{P}u^{\alpha}_{-})=&(u^{0})^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-h}c^{2}_{s}W^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(u^{0})^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-h}\big\{{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}-{2}{W}^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u^{\alpha}{W}^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}\\
&-{2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}w^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\big\}.\end{split}

(2.85)

By the commutation rule, we get

\mathbf{T}(\mathbf{P}u^{\alpha}_{-})=\mathbf{P}(\mathbf{T}u^{\alpha}_{-})+[\mathbf{T},\mathbf{P}]u^{\alpha}_{-}.

(2.86)

By (
1.24
), we see
\mathbf{P}=\mathrm{I}-(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}
. So we can compute

\begin{split}[\mathbf{T},\mathbf{P}]u^{\alpha}_{-}=&(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}-(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}_{-}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}\{(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\kappa}\}\\
&-(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial^{2}_{\beta\kappa}u^{\alpha}_{-}-(m^{\beta\gamma}+2u^{\beta}u^{\gamma})\partial_{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial^{2}_{\kappa\gamma}u^{\alpha}_{-}.\end{split}

(2.87)

Combining (
2.85
), (
2.86
), and (
2.87
), we can obtain (
2.83
). It remains for us to estimate (
2.84
).

By the space-time elliptic estimates (
1.25
), and (
2.83
), (
1.18
), and Lemma
2.4
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
&+\|\nabla^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\mathbf{u}_{-}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\mathbf{u}_{-}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|dh\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-1}}.\end{split}

(2.88)

By using (
1.24
), Sobolev imbeddings, and elliptic estimates, we can derive that

\begin{split}\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-1}}\leq\|\mathrm{e}^{-h}\mathbf{W}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-2}}&\lesssim(1+\|h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s-1}}.\end{split}

(2.89)

Combining (
2.88
), (
2.89
), we therefore conclude

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{u}_{-}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}(\|(u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}},h)\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|(u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}},h)\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}).\end{split}

At this stage, we have finished the proof of Lemma
2.19
.
∎

2.4.2.
Space elliptic estimates for vorticity

Let us introduce a decomposition of
\Delta\mathbf{w}
and
\Delta\mathbf{W}
. It plays an important role in the energy part of
\mathbf{w}
. Using the following decomposition and (
1.18
), the energy bound of
\mathbf{w}
can be transferred to estimate
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathbf{G}
through
\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{w}
and
\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{W}
.

Lemma 2.20
.

Let the one-form
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.15
). Then we have

\begin{split}\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j}=&\epsilon_{ji\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}-u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}+u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}-u_{i}w_{j}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u_{j}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+u_{j}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}.\end{split}

(2.90)

Proof.

By Lemma
2.12
, we have

\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j}=\epsilon_{ji\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}+u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{i}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{j}w_{\kappa}+u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w_{j}-u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}w_{i}.

(2.91)

For
u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}=0
, we can obtain

u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{i}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{j}w_{\kappa}=-u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}+u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}.

(2.92)

Substituting (
2.92
) and (
2.17
) to (
2.91
), it follows

\begin{split}\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j}=&\epsilon_{ji\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w}-u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}+u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}-u_{i}w_{j}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u_{j}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+u_{j}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}.\end{split}

So we have proved (
2.90
). ∎

Lemma 2.21
.

Let
W
be defined in (
1.18
). Then we have

\begin{split}\partial_{i}W_{j}-\partial_{j}W_{i}=&\epsilon_{ij\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{W}-u_{i}W_{\kappa}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}+u_{j}W_{\kappa}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}\\
&+u_{j}\big\{{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}-{2}{W}_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u_{i}{W}^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}-{2}\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}\\
&-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}\\
&+\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\big\}\\
&-u_{i}\big\{{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}-{2}{W}_{j}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u_{j}{W}^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}-{2}\epsilon_{j}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}\\
&-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}w_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-\epsilon_{j}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}\\
&+\epsilon_{j}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon_{j}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\big\}.\end{split}

(2.93)

Proof.

Firstly, using Lemma
2.12
, we can derive

\displaystyle\partial_{i}W_{j}-\partial_{j}W_{i}=\epsilon_{ij\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{W}+u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{j}W_{\kappa}-u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{i}W_{\kappa}+u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}W_{i}-u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}W_{j}.

(2.94)

Secondly, from the definition of
\mathbf{W}
, it yields

u_{\kappa}W^{\kappa}=\epsilon^{\kappa\alpha\beta\gamma}u_{\alpha}u_{\kappa}(-\partial_{\beta}w_{\gamma}+c^{-2}w_{\gamma}\partial_{\beta}h)=0.

(2.95)

By (
2.95
), we infer

\displaystyle u_{i}u^{\kappa}\partial_{j}W_{\kappa}-u_{j}u^{\kappa}\partial_{i}W_{\kappa}=

\displaystyle-u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}W_{\kappa}+u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}W_{\kappa}.

(2.96)

Substituting (
2.96
) and (
2.15
) to (
2.94
), we can obtain (
2.93
).
∎

We are ready to introduce a decomposition of
\Delta\mathbf{w}
.

Lemma 2.22
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{u})
be a solution of (
1.13
). Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.15
). Then we have

\begin{split}\left(\delta^{mj}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\right)\partial_{j}\partial_{m}w_{i}=&(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}(\epsilon_{mi\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w})+\partial^{j}(\epsilon_{ji\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w})\\
&-\partial^{j}(u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})-\partial_{i}(w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)+\partial^{j}(u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})\\
&-\partial^{j}(u_{i}u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)+\partial^{j}(u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j})-\partial^{j}(u_{i}w_{j}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})\\
&+\partial^{j}(u_{j}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)-\partial^{j}(u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i})+\partial_{j}(u_{j}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})\\
&+\partial_{i}(w_{m}\partial_{t}\left\{(u^{0})^{-1}u^{m}\right\})-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j})\partial_{j}w_{m}\\
&-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha})\\
&-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}\big\{-u_{m}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}\\
&+u_{i}\partial_{m}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{m}-u_{i}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\\
&+u_{m}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+u_{m}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\big\}.\end{split}

(2.97)

Proof.

To prove (
2.97
), we derive the formulation of
\Delta w_{i}
by

\displaystyle\Delta w_{i}=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j})+\partial_{i}\partial^{j}w_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j})+\partial_{i}(\partial^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}-\partial_{t}w^{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}w_{i}-\partial_{i}w_{j})-\partial_{i}(w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+\partial_{t}w^{0}).

(2.98)

For
u_{\kappa}w^{\kappa}=0
, so we get

w^{0}=-(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}w_{i}.

(2.99)

By (
2.99
), we can calculate

\displaystyle\partial_{t}w^{0}=\partial_{t}\{-(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}w_{i})\}=-w_{i}\partial_{t}\left\{(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\right\}-(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\partial_{t}w_{i}.

(2.100)

By using (
2.17
), we infer

\partial_{t}w_{i}=-(u^{0})^{-1}u^{j}\partial_{j}w_{i}-(u^{0})^{-1}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+(u^{0})^{-1}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}-(u^{0})^{-1}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}.

(2.101)

Substituting (
2.101
) to (
2.100
), it follows

\begin{split}\partial_{t}w^{0}=&-w_{i}\partial_{t}\left\{(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\right\}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}w_{m}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}.\end{split}

(2.102)

Inserting (
2.90
) and (
2.102
) to (
2.4.2
), we obtain

\begin{split}\Delta w_{i}=&\partial^{j}(\epsilon_{ji\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w})-\partial^{j}(u_{j}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})+\partial^{j}(u_{i}\partial_{j}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa})\\
&-\partial^{j}(u_{i}u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)+\partial^{j}(u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j})-\partial^{j}(u_{i}w_{j}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})\\
&+\partial^{j}(u_{j}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)-\partial^{j}(u_{j}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i})+\partial^{j}(u_{j}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa})\\
&-\partial_{i}(w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)+\partial_{i}(w_{m}\partial_{t}\left\{(u^{0})^{-1}u^{m}\right\})-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}w_{m})\\
&-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h)-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha})-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}).\end{split}

(2.103)

Calculate
-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}w_{m})
by

\displaystyle-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}w_{m})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}w_{m}-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j})\partial_{j}w_{m},

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}\partial_{m}w_{i}-(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}(\partial_{i}w_{m}-\partial_{m}w_{i})-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j})\partial_{j}w_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}\partial_{m}w_{i}-\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j})\partial_{j}w_{m}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}(\epsilon_{mi\gamma\delta}u^{\gamma}\mathrm{vort}^{\delta}\mathbf{w})

\displaystyle+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j}\partial_{j}\{-u_{m}\partial_{i}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}+u_{i}\partial_{m}u^{\kappa}w_{\kappa}-u_{i}u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h+u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{m}

(2.104)

\displaystyle-u_{i}w_{m}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}+u_{m}u_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h-u_{m}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+u_{m}w_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\}.

Inserting (
2.4.2
) to (
2.103
), then we can obtain (
2.97
). Therefore, we have proved Lemma
2.22
.
∎

Lemma 2.23
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{u})
be a solution of (
1.13
). Let
\mathbf{w}
,
\mathbf{W}
, and
\mathring{\mathbf{W}}
be defined in (
1.15
), (
1.18
), (
1.19
). Then we have the following estimate

\begin{split}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.105)

Proof.

To derive the formulation of
\Delta W_{i}
, we calculate

\displaystyle\Delta W_{i}=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}W_{i}-\partial_{i}W_{j})+\partial_{i}\partial^{j}W_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}W_{i}-\partial_{i}W_{j})+\partial_{i}(\partial^{\alpha}W_{\alpha})-\partial_{i}(\partial^{0}W_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial^{j}(\partial_{j}W_{i}-\partial_{i}W_{j})+\partial_{i}(\partial^{\alpha}W_{\alpha})-\partial_{i}(\partial_{t}W^{0}).

(2.106)

By (
2.4.2
), we infer

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1,2,3}\|\partial_{j}W_{i}-\partial_{i}W_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\partial^{\alpha}W_{\alpha}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\partial_{t}W^{0}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.

(2.107)

By (
2.93
), it yields

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum_{i,j=1,2,3}}\|\partial_{j}W_{i}-\partial_{i}W_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{W}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.108)

A direct computation tells us

u_{\alpha}W^{\alpha}=-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\delta}+c_{s}^{-2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\alpha}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h=0,

so we get

W^{0}=-(u_{0})^{-1}u^{i}W_{i}.

(2.109)

Due to (
2.109
), so we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}W^{0}=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\{-(u_{0})^{-1}u^{i}W_{i})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-W_{i}\partial_{t}\left\{(u_{0})^{-1}u^{i}\right\}-(u_{0})^{-1}u^{i}\partial_{t}W_{i}.

(2.110)

By using (
2.4.2
), we can obtain

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}W^{0}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq\|\mathbf{W}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}|}{|u_{0}|}\|\partial_{t}\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.111)

By using (
2.15
) and (
1.13
), we get

\begin{split}\partial_{t}{W}_{i}=&-(u_{0})^{-1}u^{j}\partial_{j}{W}_{i}+{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}-{2}(u_{0})^{-1}{W}_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}-\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&+(u_{0})^{-1}u_{i}{W}^{\beta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{\beta}-{2}\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}(u_{0})^{-1}w_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\\
&+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h\\
=&-(u_{0})^{-1}u^{j}\partial_{j}{W}_{i}+{W}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}-{2}(u_{0})^{-1}{W}_{i}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}-\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&-(u_{0})^{-1}u_{i}{W}^{\beta}\partial_{\beta}h-{2}\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}\partial_{\delta}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\kappa}-{2}\mathrm{e}^{-h}(u_{0})^{-1}w_{i}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h\\
&-\epsilon^{\kappa\beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u_{i}+\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}c^{-2}_{s}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}\\
&+(c^{-2}_{s}+2)\epsilon_{i}^{\ \beta\gamma\delta}(u_{0})^{-1}u_{\beta}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\delta}u_{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}h.\end{split}

(2.112)

Due to (
2.112
), we have

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq&\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}|}{|u_{0}|}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.113)

Combining (
2.111
) and (
2.113
), it follows

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}W^{0}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq&\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}}{|u_{0}|^{2}}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.114)

On the other hand, we can also derive

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}W^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\left(-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\delta}+c_{s}^{-2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}w_{\delta}+c_{s}^{-2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}w_{\delta}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}h+c_{s}^{-2}\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}w_{\delta}\partial_{\gamma}h.

(2.115)

Due to (
2.4.2
), we get

\begin{split}\|\partial_{\alpha}W^{\alpha}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.116)

Inserting (
2.108
), (
2.114
), and (
2.116
) to (
2.107
), so we get

\begin{split}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq&\frac{|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}}{|u_{0}|^{2}}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(2.117)

By (
1.1
), we can see

\begin{split}|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}|^{2}=|u_{0}|^{2}-1.\end{split}

(2.118)

Using (
2.117
) and (
2.118
), we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{|u_{0}|^{2}}\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\leq&C\|\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

For
1\leq|u_{0}|\leq 2+C_{0}
, we can obtain (
2.105
).
∎

3.
Energy estimates

In this part, our goal is to prove the energy estimates for Theorem
1.2
and
1.3
. Firstly, we introduce a classical energy estimate for density and velocity.

Theorem 3.1

(Classical energy estimate,
[
13
]
)
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{u})
be a solution of (
1.13
). Then for any
a\geq 0
, we have

\|h\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{a}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{a}}\lesssim\left(\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{a}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{a}}\right)\exp({\int^{t}_{0}}\|d\mathring{\mathbf{u}},dh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau),\quad t\in[0,T].

(3.1)

Remark 3.1
.

(1) Due to (
1.1
), we can get

u^{0}=\sqrt{(u^{1})^{2}+(u^{2})^{2}+(u^{3})^{2}+1},\quad u_{0}=-\sqrt{(u^{1})^{2}+(u^{2})^{2}+(u^{3})^{2}+1}.

(3.2)

By using classical energy estimates for the symmetric hyperbolic system (
2.12
), and combining with (
1.12
), Lemma
2.3
, we can obtain (
3.1
).

(2) For
a\geq 1
, using (
2.12
) and (
3.1
) again, we therefore have

\|(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\|_{H_{x}^{a-1}}\lesssim\|(\nabla h,\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\|_{H_{x}^{a-1}}.

(3.3)

By (
3.2
) and (
3.3
), it follows

\|\partial_{t}u^{0}\|_{H_{x}^{a-1}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}\|_{H_{x}^{a-1}}\lesssim\|(\nabla h,\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\|_{H_{x}^{a-1}}.

(3.4)

3.1.
Energy estimate for Theorem
1.2

Theorem 3.2

(Energy estimate: type 1)
.

Let
(h,\mathbf{u})
be a solution of (
1.13
). Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.15
) and
\mathbf{w}
satisfy (
2.17
)-(
2.18
). For
2<s_{0}<s\leq\frac{5}{2}
, the following energy estimate

E_{s}(t)\leq E_{0}\exp\left(5M(t)\cdot\mathrm{e}^{5M(t)}\right),

(3.5)

holds, where

\begin{split}&M(t)={\int^{t}_{0}}(\|d\mathbf{u},dh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{u},dh\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}})d\tau,\\
&E_{s}(t)=\|h\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|(\mathring{\mathbf{u}},u^{0}-1)\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}},\\
&E_{0}=C\left(\|(h_{0},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}+\|(h_{0},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0})\|^{10}_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{10}_{H^{s_{0}}}\right).\end{split}

(3.6)

Above,
C
is also a universal constant.

Proof.

To prove (
3.5
), we still need to bound
\|u^{0}-1\|_{{H}_{x}^{s}}
and
\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}
. By using (
3.2
) and (
3.1
), we obtain

\|u^{0}-1\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{s}}(t)\lesssim\|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\left(\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\right)\exp({\int^{t}_{0}}\|d\mathring{\mathbf{u}},dh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau).

(3.7)

For
\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}
, we consider

\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}=\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}.

(3.8)

So it’s natural for us to utilize the transport structure of
\mathbf{w}
and the higher derivatives
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathbf{G}
. Multiplying
(u^{0})^{-1}w^{\alpha}
on (
2.17
) and integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}=&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}((u^{0})^{-1}u^{i})|\mathbf{w}|^{2}dx-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\alpha}w^{\alpha}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}hdx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u^{0})^{-1}w^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\alpha}w^{\alpha}dx-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u^{0})^{-1}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa}|\mathbf{w}|^{2}dx.\end{split}

(3.9)

By Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim&(\|dh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.10)

Integrating (
3.10
) on the time interval
[0,t]
(
t>0
), so we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}(t,\cdot)\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|dh,d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.11)

For
\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}}}
, we notice

\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}=\|\Delta\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.

(3.12)

Since
\mathbf{w}=(w^{0},\mathring{\mathbf{w}})
, let us first consider
\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}
. Set the operator

\Delta_{H}=(\delta^{mj}+(u^{0})^{-2}u^{m}u^{j})\partial_{m}\partial_{j}.

(3.13)

By (
1.1
),
\Delta_{H}
is also an elliptic operator on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Therefore, we deduce that

\|\Delta_{H}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim\|\Delta\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{H}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.

(3.14)

Due t0 (
2.97
), we have

\|\Delta_{H}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.

(3.15)

Since (
1.18
), (
2.99
), and Lemma
2.4
, it follows

\begin{split}\|\Delta_{H}\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.\end{split}

(3.16)

Combining (
3.14
), (
3.15
) and (
3.16
), we infer

\begin{split}\|\Delta\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.\end{split}

(3.17)

Seeing from (
2.99
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|\Delta{w^{0}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\Delta\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\Delta\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(3.18)

By Hölder’s inequality, then (
3.18
) becomes

\begin{split}\|\Delta{w^{0}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\Delta\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.\end{split}

(3.19)

Inserting (
3.17
) to (
3.19
), we have

\begin{split}\|\Delta\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.\end{split}

(3.20)

By using (
1.18
), (
2.17
), and (
2.99
), we can also prove

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{u}\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\Delta\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.

(3.21)

Seeing from (
3.20
) and (
3.1
), there is only a lower order term in
\|\Delta\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}
and
\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}
. So we transfer the goal to bound
\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}
.

Step 1:
\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}
. We also first consider
\|\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}
. On one hand, we have

\|\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}=\|\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.

(3.22)

By (
2.105
), (
3.22
) and (
1.18
), we therefore get

\begin{split}\|\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{W}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}.\end{split}

(3.23)

Using (
1.18
), (
2.100
), (
2.101
), (
1.15
) and Hölder’s inequality, then (
3.23
) becomes

\begin{split}\|\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|d\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\nabla\mathbf{w}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d\mathbf{u}\cdot dh\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}).\end{split}

(3.24)

Since (
2.109
), so it’s easy for us to get

\begin{split}\|{W}^{0}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\lesssim&\|\mathring{\mathbf{W}}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}.\end{split}

(3.25)

Combining (
3.24
) and (
3.25
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}).\end{split}

(3.26)

Due to (
3.12
), (
3.20
) and (
3.26
), it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}).\end{split}

By interpolation formula and Young’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}})\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\frac{1}{10}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}})\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\frac{1}{10}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}+C\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}).\end{split}

(3.27)

Therefore, we turn to bound
\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}}
as follows.

Step 2:
\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}}
. By (
2.28
), we have

\begin{split}(u^{0})^{-1}u^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\left(G^{\alpha}-F^{\alpha}\right)=&(u^{0})^{-1}E^{\alpha}+\partial^{\alpha}\left((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\right)-\Gamma\partial^{\alpha}\left((u^{0})^{-1}\right).\end{split}

(3.28)

Operating
\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}
on (
3.28
), we have

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}\left(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G^{\alpha}-F^{\alpha})\right)+(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\partial_{i}\left(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G^{\alpha}-F^{\alpha})\right)\\
=&\partial^{\alpha}\left(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma)\right)-[\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2},(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\partial_{i}](G^{\alpha}-F^{\alpha})\\
&+\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\left((u^{0})^{-1}E^{\alpha}\right)-\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\Gamma\partial^{\alpha}\left((u^{0})^{-1}\right)\right\}.\end{split}

(3.29)

Multiplying
\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G_{\alpha}-F_{\alpha})
on (
3.29
) and integrating it on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we can derive that

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{F})\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}=&\mathrm{H}_{1}+\mathrm{H}_{2}+\mathrm{H}_{3}+\mathrm{H}_{4},\end{split}

(3.30)

where we record

\displaystyle\mathrm{H}_{1}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\right\}\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G_{\alpha}-F_{\alpha})dx,

(3.31)

\displaystyle\mathrm{H}_{2}={\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\left\{(u^{0})^{-1}E^{\alpha}\right\}\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G_{\alpha}-F_{\alpha})dx,

(3.32)

\displaystyle\mathrm{H}_{3}=-{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}[\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2},(u^{0})^{-1}u^{i}\partial_{i}](G^{\alpha}-F^{\alpha})\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G_{\alpha}-F_{\alpha})dx,

(3.33)

\displaystyle\mathrm{H}_{4}=-{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\Gamma\partial^{\alpha}\left((u^{0})^{-1}\right)\right\}\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G_{\alpha}-F_{\alpha})dx.

(3.34)

For
\mathrm{H}_{1}
is the most difficult term, so we will discuss it later.

The bound for
\mathrm{H}_{2},\mathrm{H}_{3},\mathrm{H}_{4}
.
To get the bound of
\mathrm{H}_{2},\mathrm{H}_{3},\mathrm{H}_{4}
, commutator estimates and Hölder’s inequality are enough. By Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\mathrm{H}_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}(\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}).

(3.35)

So we need to know the estimate of
\mathbf{F}
,
\mathbf{E}
and
\mathbf{G}
. Recalling (
2.30
), and using Hölder’s inequality, then it follows

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}).

(3.36)

Seeing from (
2.31
), and using Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
2.5
, it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{E}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot d^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot(d^{2}\mathbf{u},d^{2}h)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\\
&+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.37)

By (
1.18
), we compute out

\displaystyle G^{\alpha}=\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\epsilon_{\delta}^{\ \eta\mu\nu}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\left(u_{\eta}\partial_{\mu}w_{\nu}-c^{-2}_{s}u_{\eta}w_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}h\right).

(3.38)

Due to (
3.38
), using Hölder’s inequality, we infer

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{G}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\lesssim&\|d^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot(d^{2}\mathbf{u},d^{2}h)\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
&+\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.39)

Inserting (
3.1
), (
3.37
), (
3.39
) to (
3.35
), we therefore obtain

\begin{split}|\mathrm{H}_{2}|\lesssim&(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}})\\
&+(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{5}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.40)

By Lemma
2.10
, we can derive

\displaystyle|\mathrm{H}_{3}|\leq

\displaystyle\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{4}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}).

(3.41)

For
\mathrm{H}_{4}
, seeing (
2.29
), (
3.1
), (
3.39
), and using Hölder’s inequality, we can show that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{H}_{4}|\leq

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\cdot d\mathbf{u}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\|\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}).

(3.42)

The bound for
\mathrm{H}_{1}
. If we take the derivatives in
\Gamma
directly, there will be a loss of derivative through Hölder’s inequality. So we seek to capture the cancellation of highest derivatives from integrating by parts. Therefore, we divide
\mathrm{H}_{1}
by two parts

\begin{split}\mathrm{H}_{1}=&\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big))\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}G^{\alpha}dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{11}}\underbrace{-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big))\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}F^{\alpha}dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{12}}.\end{split}

(3.43)

Consider

\begin{split}\partial_{\alpha}G^{\alpha}=\partial_{\alpha}(-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}W_{\delta})=-\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}W_{\delta}.\end{split}

(3.44)

For
\mathrm{H}_{11}
, integrating it parts, which yields

\begin{split}\mathrm{H}_{11}=&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}(\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}G^{\alpha})dx-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{\alpha}G^{\alpha})dx\\
=&\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}G^{0}dx\underbrace{-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{\alpha}G^{\alpha})dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{111}}.\end{split}

(3.45)

By using (
3.44
), we infer

\mathrm{H}_{111}=\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}W_{\delta})dx.

Similarly, we can calculate
\mathrm{H}_{12}
by

\begin{split}\mathrm{H}_{12}=&-\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}F^{0}dx+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{\alpha}F^{\alpha})dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{121}}.\end{split}

(3.46)

Inserting (
3.43
), (
3.45
) and (
3.46
) to (
3.30
), it follows

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{F})\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(G^{0}-F^{0})dx\right)=&\mathrm{H}_{111}+\mathrm{H}_{121}+\mathrm{H}_{3}+\mathrm{H}_{4}.\end{split}

(3.47)

On the right hand side of (
3.47
), we have estimated
\mathrm{H}_{3},\mathrm{H}_{4}
by (
3.1
)-(
3.1
). It still remains for us to consider
\mathrm{H}_{111}
and
\mathrm{H}_{121}
.

The bound for
\mathrm{H}_{111}
.
Using Lemma
2.5
, Hölder’s inequality, (
2.29
) and (
1.18
), we infer

\begin{split}|\mathrm{H}_{111}|\lesssim&\|\Gamma\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot d\mathbf{W}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}(\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot(d\mathbf{u},dh)\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\cdot d^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|\mathbf{w}\cdot d^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-2}})\\
\lesssim&(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\\
&+(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.48)

The bound for
\mathrm{H}_{121}
.
Due to (
2.30
), and
u_{\lambda}w^{\lambda}=0
, we can compute out

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}F^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}(-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}hW_{\delta}-2u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}+2u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}(-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}hW_{\delta}-2u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-2\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}hW_{\delta}-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\alpha}W_{\delta}-2\partial_{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}

\displaystyle-2w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}-2w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}-2w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\alpha}u^{\gamma}

\displaystyle-2\partial_{\alpha}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-2u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}-2u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}

\displaystyle+4c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}h\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}.

To be simple, we record
\partial_{\alpha}F^{\alpha}
as

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}F^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\Psi-2u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda},

(3.49)

where

\begin{split}\Psi=&-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}hW_{\delta}-2\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}c_{s}^{-2}u_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}h\partial_{\alpha}W_{\delta}-2\partial_{\alpha}w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\\
&-2w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}-2w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u^{\gamma}-2w^{\lambda}\partial^{\alpha}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}\partial_{\alpha}u^{\gamma}-2\partial_{\alpha}u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\\
&-2u^{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}+4c_{s}^{-3}c^{\prime}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}h\partial_{\lambda}h\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}-2c_{s}^{-2}\partial_{\lambda}h\partial_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\lambda}.\end{split}

(3.50)

Due to (
3.50
) and (
1.18
), using Lemma
2.5
and Hölder’s inequality, we can bound

\begin{split}\|\Psi\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot d^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot d\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\\
&+\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot(d^{2}h,d^{2}\mathbf{u})\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot\mathbf{w}\cdot(dh,d\mathbf{u})\cdot(dh,d\mathbf{u})\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\\
&+(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u},dh)\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}),\end{split}

(3.51)

For
\mathrm{H}_{121}
, using (
3.49
) and (
3.50
), we have

\begin{split}\mathrm{H}_{121}=&\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\Psi dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{12a}}-\underbrace{2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}((u^{0})^{-1}\Gamma)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(u^{\alpha}\partial^{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}u_{\lambda})dx}_{\equiv\mathrm{H}_{12b}}\\
