\begin{split}&\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}h_{m}=h\ \ \mathrm{in}\ \ H^{\sigma}_{x},\ \ 0\leq\sigma<s,\\
&\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\mathbf{w}_{m}=\mathbf{w}\ \ \mathrm{in}\ \ H^{\theta}_{x},\ \ 0\leq\theta<s_{0}.\end{split}

(7.2)

It remains for us to prove the convergence with the highest derivatives of
(\mathbf{u}_{m},h_{m},\mathbf{w}_{m})
.

Step 2: Constructing smooth solutions
. We set
\mathbf{U}_{0}=(p(h_{0}),u^{1}_{0},u^{2}_{0},u^{3}_{0},)^{\mathrm{T}}
. By
[
58
,
24
]
, there exists a sharp frequency envelope for
u^{0}_{0}-1,u^{1}_{0},u^{2}_{0},u^{3}_{0}
and
h_{0}
respectively. Let
\{c^{(a)}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
(
a=0,1,2,3,
) be a sharp frequency envelope respectively for
u^{0}_{0}-1,u^{1}_{0},u^{2}_{0}
, and
u^{3}_{0}
in the space
H^{s}
. Let
\{c^{(4)}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
be a sharp frequency envelope respectively for
h_{0}
in
H^{s_{0}}
. Let
d^{(0)}_{k},d^{(1)}_{k},d^{(2)}_{k},d^{(3)}_{k}
be a sharp frequency envelope for
w^{0}_{0},w^{1}_{0},w^{2}_{0}
and
w^{3}_{0}
in the space
H^{s_{0}}
. We choose a family of regularizations
\mathbf{U}^{l}_{0}=(p(h^{l}_{0}),u^{1l}_{0},u^{2l}_{0},u^{3l}_{0})^{\mathrm{T}}\in\cap^{\infty}_{a=0}H^{a}
at frequencies
\lesssim 2^{l}
where
l
is a frequency parameter and
l\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
. Denote

u^{0l}_{0}=\sqrt{1+\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0}^{l}},\quad\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0}=(u^{0l}_{0},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0}^{l})^{\mathrm{T}},\quad\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{h^{l}_{0}}\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0}).

Then the functions
u^{0l}_{0},u^{1l}_{0},u^{2l}_{0},u^{3l}_{0},h^{l}_{0}
, and
\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0}=(w^{0l}_{0},w^{1l}_{0},w^{2l}_{0},w^{3l}_{0})^{\mathrm{T}}
have the following properties:

(i) uniform bounds

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(u^{0l}_{0}-1)\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(0)}_{k},\qquad\|P_{k}u^{il}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(i)}_{k},\qquad\quad i=1,2,3,\\
&\|P_{k}h^{l}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(4)}_{k},\qquad\qquad\|P_{k}w^{\alpha l}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\lesssim d^{(\alpha)}_{k},\qquad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\end{split}

(7.3)

(ii) high frequency bounds

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(u^{0l}_{0}-1)\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(0)}_{k},\qquad\|P_{k}u^{il}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(i)}_{k},\qquad\quad i=1,2,3,\\
&\|P_{k}h^{l}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(4)}_{k},\qquad\qquad\|P_{k}w^{\alpha l}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}d^{(\alpha)}_{k},\qquad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\end{split}

(7.4)

(iii) difference bounds

\begin{split}&\|u^{\alpha(l+1)}_{0}-u^{\alpha l}_{0}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c^{(\alpha)}_{l},\quad\ \ \ \alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
&\|w^{\alpha(l+1)}_{0}-w^{\alpha l}_{0}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-s_{0}l}d^{(\alpha)}_{l},\quad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
&\|h^{l+1}_{0}-h^{l}_{0}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-{s}l}c^{(4)}_{l},\end{split}

(7.5)

(iv) limit

\begin{split}&\lim_{l\rightarrow\infty}(h_{0}^{l},\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0},\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0})=(h_{0},\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\ \ \ \mathrm{in}\ \ H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{s_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.6)

Consider the Cauchy problem (
1.20
) with the initial data

\begin{split}&(h^{l},\mathbf{u}^{l},\mathbf{w}^{l})|_{t=0}=(h^{l}_{0},\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0},\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0}).\end{split}

By Proposition
4.1
, we can obtain a family of smooth solutions
(h^{l},\mathbf{u}^{l},\mathbf{w}^{l})
on a time interval
[0,T]
(
T>0
). Furthermore, based on (
7.3
)-(
7.6
), using Proposition
4.1
again, we can claim that

\bullet
high frequency bounds

\|u^{0l}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}\lesssim 2^{l}M_{0},

(7.7)

\bullet
difference bounds

\|(\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c_{l},

(7.8)

and

\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(s-1)l}c_{l},

(7.9)

and

\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\lesssim c_{l},

(7.10)

where
c_{l}=\sum^{3}_{\alpha=0}c^{(a)}_{l}+c^{(4)}_{l}+\sum^{3}_{\alpha=0}d^{(\alpha)}_{l}
. By using (
7.4
) and Proposition
4.1
, then the estimate (
7.7
) holds. Let us postpone to prove (
7.8
)-(
7.10
). By using

h-h^{l}=\sum^{\infty}_{k=l}(h^{k+1}-h^{k}),

and the estimate (
7.8
), we can conclude

\|h-h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c_{\geq l}.

(7.11)

Here
c_{\geq l}=\sum_{j\geq l}c_{j}
. Similarly, by using (
7.8
), (
7.9
) and (
7.10
), we also have

\|\mathbf{u}-\mathbf{u}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c_{\geq l},\quad\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\lesssim c_{\geq l}.

(7.12)

Step 3: Convergence of the highest derivatives
. For the initial data
(\mathbf{u}_{0m},h_{0m},\mathbf{w}_{0m})
and
\mathbf{u}_{0m}=(u^{0}_{0m},u^{1}_{0m},u^{2}_{0m},u^{3}_{0m})
, let
\{c^{(i)m}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
be frequency envelopes for the initial data
u^{i}_{0m}
in
H^{s}(i=1,2,3)
. Let
\{c^{(4)m}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
be frequency envelopes for
u^{0}_{0m}-1
in
H^{s}
. Let
\{d^{(\alpha)m}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
be frequency envelopes for the initial data
w^{\alpha}_{0m}
in
H^{s_{0}}(\alpha=0,1,2,3)
. We choose a family of regularizations
(u^{1l}_{0m},u^{2l}_{0m},u^{3l}_{0m},h^{l}_{0m})\in\cap^{\infty}_{a=0}H^{a}
at frequencies
\lesssim 2^{l}
. Denote

u^{0l}_{0m}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}_{0m}|^{2}},\quad\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0m}=(u^{0l}_{0m},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}_{0m})^{\mathrm{T}},\quad\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0m}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{h^{l}_{0m}}\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0m}).

Then,
\mathbf{u}^{l}_{0m}
,
h^{l}_{0m}
, and
\mathbf{w}^{l}_{0m}
satisfy

(1) uniform bounds

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(u^{0l}_{0m}-1)\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(0)m}_{k},\quad\|P_{k}u^{il}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(i)m}_{k},\ \quad i=1,2,3,\\
&\|P_{k}h^{l}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c^{(4)m}_{k},\qquad\|P_{k}w^{\alpha l}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\lesssim d^{(\alpha)m}_{k},\qquad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\end{split}

(2) high frequency bounds

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(u^{0l}_{0m}-1)\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(0)}_{k},\quad\|P_{k}u^{il}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(i)}_{k},\quad i=1,2,3,\\
&\|P_{k}h^{l}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\lesssim 2^{k}c^{(4)}_{k},\qquad\|P_{k}w^{\alpha l}_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\lesssim 2^{k}d^{(\alpha)}_{k},\qquad\ \alpha=0,1,2,3,\end{split}

(3) difference bounds

\begin{split}&\|u^{\alpha(l+1)}_{0m}-u^{\alpha l}_{0m}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c^{(\alpha)m}_{l},\quad\ \ \alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
&\|w^{\alpha(l+1)}_{0m}-w^{\alpha l}_{0m}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-s_{0}l}d^{(\alpha)m}_{l},\quad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
&\|h^{l+1}_{0m}-h^{l}_{0m}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c^{(4)m}_{l},\end{split}

(4) limit

\begin{split}&\lim_{l\rightarrow\infty}(h^{l}_{0m},\mathbf{u}_{0m}^{l},\mathbf{w}_{0m}^{l})=(h_{0m},\mathbf{u}_{0m},\mathbf{w}_{0m})\ \mathrm{in}\ H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{s_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.13)

By Proposition
4.1
, we can obtain a family of smooth solutions
(h_{j}^{l},\mathbf{u}_{j}^{l},\mathbf{w}_{j}^{l})
on a time interval
28
28

28

For the initial sequence is uniformly bounded, then the time interval
[0,T]
can be independent for
j
and
l
.

[0,T]
with the initial data
(h_{0m}^{l},\mathbf{u}_{0m}^{l},\mathbf{w}_{0m}^{l})
. Let us now prove

\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}h_{m}=h,\quad\mathrm{in}\ \ H^{s}_{x}.

Inserting
h^{l}_{m}
into
h_{m}-h
, we have

\begin{split}\|h_{m}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\leq&\|h^{l}_{m}-h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|h^{l}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|h^{l}_{m}-h_{m}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}.\end{split}

(7.14)

Due to (
7.13
), we have

\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}h^{l}_{0m}=h^{l}_{0}\quad\mathrm{in}\ {H^{\sigma}_{x}},\ 0\leq\sigma<\infty.

(7.15)

Using similar idea of the proof of (
7.2
), we can derive that

\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}h^{l}_{m}=h^{l}\quad\mathrm{in}\ {H^{\sigma}_{x}},\ 0\leq\sigma<\infty.

(7.16)

From (
7.15
), we have

c^{(4)j}_{k}\rightarrow c^{(4)}_{k},\ j\rightarrow\infty.

(7.17)

By (
7.11
), we then bound (
7.14
) by

\begin{split}\|h_{m}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\|h^{l}_{m}-h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+c_{\geq l}+c^{(4)m}_{\geq l},\end{split}

(7.18)

Taking the limit of (
7.18
) for
j\rightarrow\infty
, by using (
7.16
) and (
7.17
), it leads to

\begin{split}\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\|h_{m}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim c_{\geq l}+c^{(4)}_{\geq l}\lesssim c_{\geq l}.\end{split}

(7.19)

Finally, taking
l\rightarrow\infty
for (
7.19
) and using
\lim_{l\rightarrow\infty}c_{\geq l}=0
, we have

\begin{split}\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\|h_{m}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}=0.\end{split}

Similarly, by using (
7.8
), (
7.9
), and (
7.10
), we can also obtain

\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{u}_{m}-\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}=0,\quad\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{w}_{m}-\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}=0.

Step 4: Proof of (
7.7
)-(
7.10
)
.
Firstly, we can obtain (
7.7
) by energy estimates and Strichartz estimate in Proposition
5.13
. To prove (
7.8
)-(
7.10
), we also need the hyperbolic system to discuss
\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l}
and
h^{l+1}-h^{l}
. But for
\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}
, we use it’s transport equations of
\mathbf{w}^{l+1}
and
\mathbf{w}^{l}
. However, if we consider the difference terms
(\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l},\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l})
, then the original system is destroyed or it’s a disturbed system. To avoid the loss of derivatives of
\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l}
and
h^{l+1}-h^{l}
, the Strichartz estimates in Proposition
5.13
plays a key role. While, the difference
\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}
is weaker than
\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l}
for there is no dispersion.

Let
\mathbf{U}^{l}=(p(h^{l}),u^{1l},u^{2l},u^{3l})^{\mathrm{T}}
. Then
\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l}
satisfies

\begin{cases}&A^{0}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1})\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l})+A^{i}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1})\partial_{i}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l})=\Pi^{l},\\
&(\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l})|_{t=0}=\mathbf{U}_{0}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}_{0}^{l},\end{cases}

(7.20)

where

\Pi^{l}=-[A^{0}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1})-A^{0}(\mathbf{U}^{l})]\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}^{l}-[A^{i}(\mathbf{U}^{l+1})-A^{i}(\mathbf{U}^{l})]\partial_{i}\mathbf{U}^{l}.

(7.21)

Multiplying with
\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l}
, integrating it on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, and using Strichartz estimates and Gronwall’s inequality, we then get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{U}^{l+1}-\mathbf{U}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{U}^{l+1}_{0}-\mathbf{U}^{l}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\exp\left(\int^{T}_{0}\|d\mathbf{U}^{l+1},d\mathbf{U}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right)\lesssim 2^{-sl}c_{l}.\end{split}

(7.22)

Hence, we have

\begin{split}\|h^{l+1}-h^{l},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l+1}-\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c_{l}.\end{split}

(7.23)

For
u^{0l}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}|^{2}}
, then we have

\begin{split}\|u^{0(l+1)}-u^{0l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-sl}c_{l}.\end{split}

(7.24)

So we have already proved (
7.8
). For
0<t\leq T
, we assume

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}L_{x}^{2}}\leq 10C2^{-(s-1)l}c_{l},\qquad\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\leq 10Cc_{l},\end{split}

(7.25)

and

\begin{split}&2^{l}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+2^{l}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T]}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\leq Cc_{l}.\end{split}

(7.26)

By using Lemma
2.15
, we get

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l})+\left((u^{0(l+1)})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l+1}\cdot\nabla\right)(\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l})\\
=&\left\{\left((u^{0(l+1)})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l+1}-(u^{0l})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}^{l}\right)\cdot\nabla\right\}\mathbf{w}^{l}\\
&-\left((u^{0(l+1)})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}^{l+1}-(u^{0l})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}^{l}\right)w^{\kappa(l+1)}\partial_{\kappa}h^{l+1}\\
&-(u^{0l})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}^{l}(w^{\kappa(l+1)}-w^{(}\kappa l))\partial_{\kappa}h^{l+1}-(u^{0l})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}^{l}w^{\kappa l}\partial_{\kappa}(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\\
&+\left((u^{0(l+1)})^{-1}-(u^{0l})^{-1}\right)w^{\kappa(j+1)}\partial_{\kappa}\mathbf{u}^{l+1}+(u^{0l})^{-1}(w^{\kappa(l+1)}-w^{\kappa l})\partial_{\kappa}\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\\
&+(u^{0l})^{-1}w_{\kappa l}\partial_{\kappa}(\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l})-\left((u^{0(l+1)})^{-1}-(u^{0l})^{-1}\right)\mathbf{w}^{l+1}\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa(j+1)}\\
&-(u^{0l})^{-1}(\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l})\partial_{\kappa}u^{\kappa(l+1)}-(u^{0l})^{-1}\mathbf{w}^{l}\partial_{\kappa}(u^{\kappa(l+1)}-u^{\kappa l})\end{split}

(7.27)

Multiplying (
7.27
) with
\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}
and integrating on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}_{0}-\mathbf{w}_{0}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh^{l+1},d\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|dh^{l+1},d\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d(h^{l+1}-h^{l}),d(\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(7.28)

By Hölder’s inequality, we can bound (
7.28
) by

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}_{0}-\mathbf{w}_{0}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh^{l+1},d\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(\|\nabla\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|dh^{l+1},d\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}})d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla(h^{l+1}-h^{l}),\nabla(\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(7.29)

Due to (
7.25
) and (
7.26
), it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq&C^{2}(2^{-sl}c_{l})^{2}+100C^{2}(2^{-sl}c_{l})^{2}2^{-\frac{l}{2}}+C^{2}(2^{-(s-1)l}c_{l})^{2}\\
&+C^{2}\textstyle{\int}^{T}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh^{l+1},d\mathbf{u}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(7.30)

Using Gronwall’s inequality for (
7.30
), we then get

\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}L_{x}^{2}}\leq 5C2^{-(s-1)l}c_{l}.

(7.31)

Similarly, we can also prove

\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\leq 5Cc_{l},

(7.32)

and

\begin{split}&2^{l}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+2^{l}\|\mathbf{u}^{l+1}-\mathbf{u}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T]}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\leq\frac{C}{2}c_{l}.\end{split}

(7.33)

Therefore, (
7.9
)-(
7.10
) hold. At this stage, we have finished the whole proof of Corollary
7.1
.
∎

8.
Proof of Theorem
1.3

Our goal is to prove Theorem
1.3
. As a first step, we reduce the proof to Proposition
8.1
. Secondly, we prove Proposition
8.1
through Proposition
8.2
. Finally, we use Proposition
4.2
to obtain Proposition
8.2
.

8.1.
Proof of Theorem
1.3
by Proposition
8.1

In Theorem
1.3
, it includes the existence, uniqueness, and continuous dependence of solutions. The key part for us is to prove the existence of solutions. To do that, we construct a sequence of smooth solutions by frequency truncation. After that, we prove the sequence converges in some Sobolev spaces. So the limit of this sequence is a solution. To find this sequence, for
j\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, we introduce the initial sequence
(\mathbf{u}_{0j},h_{0j})
by

\begin{split}&\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}=P_{\leq j}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0},\quad u^{0}_{0j}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}|^{2}},\\
&h_{0j}=P_{\leq j}h_{0},\quad\mathbf{u}_{0j}=(u^{0}_{0j},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}),\end{split}

(8.1)

where
h_{0}
and
\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0}
is stated as (
1.43
) in Theorem
1.3
, and
P_{\leq j}=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\leq j}P_{k}
. Following (
1.15
), so we define

\mathbf{w}_{0j}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{h_{0j}}\mathbf{u}_{0j}).

(8.2)

Calculate

\begin{split}w^{\alpha}_{0j}=&\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\eta}\mathrm{e}^{h_{0j}}u_{0\beta j}\partial_{\gamma}u_{0\eta j}\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{h_{0j}}u_{0\beta j}(\epsilon^{\alpha\beta\gamma\eta}\partial_{\gamma}u_{0\eta j})\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{h_{0j}}u_{0\beta j}P_{\leq j}\left(\mathrm{e}^{h_{0}}w^{\alpha}_{0}u^{\beta}_{0}-\mathrm{e}^{h_{0}}u^{\alpha}_{0}w^{\beta}_{0}\right).\end{split}

By Hölder’s inequality, then we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}(1+\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{2}}+\|\nabla{\mathbf{u}}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{1}})\\
\leq&C(M_{*}+M^{5}_{*}).\end{split}

(8.3)

Adding (
8.1
) and (
8.3
), we can see

\|\mathbf{u}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|h_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C(M_{*}+M^{5}_{*})=E(0).

(8.4)

Using (
1.34
) and (
8.1
), we get

|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j},h_{0j}|\leq C_{0},\quad 1\leq u^{0}_{0j}\leq 1+C_{0},\quad c_{s}|_{t=0}\geq c_{0}>0.

(8.5)

Before we give a proof of Theorem
1.3
, let us now introduce Proposition
8.1
.

Proposition 8.1
.

Let
s
be stated in Theorem
1.3
and (
1.34
)-(
1.43
) hold. For each
j\geq 1
, consider Cauchy problem (
1.20
) with the initial data
(h_{0j},\mathbf{u}_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j})
. Then for all
j\geq 1
, there exists two positive constants
T^{*}>0
and
{M}_{2}>0
(
T^{*}
and
{M}_{2}
only depends on
s,C_{0},c_{0}
and
{M}_{*}
) such that (
1.20
) has a unique solution
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
satisfying
(h_{j},u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s-1})
,
\mathbf{w}_{j}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{2})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{1})
. To be precise,

\mathrm{(1)}
the solution
h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j}
and
\mathbf{w}_{j}
satisfy the energy estimates

\|h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|(u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\leq{M}_{2},

(8.6)

and

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j},\partial_{t}h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{1}}\leq{M}_{2},

(8.7)

and

\|h_{j},u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq 2+C_{0},\quad u^{0}_{j}\geq 1.

(8.8)

\mathrm{(2)}
the solution
h_{j}
and
\mathbf{u}_{j}
satisfy the Strichartz estimate

\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\leq{M}_{2}.

(8.9)

\mathrm{(3)}
for
\frac{s}{2}\leq r\leq 3
, consider the following linear wave equation

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f_{j}=0,\qquad[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f_{j},\partial_{t}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(f_{0j},f_{1j}),\end{cases}

(8.10)

where
(f_{0j},f_{1j})=(P_{\leq j}f_{0},P_{\leq j}f_{1})
and
(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}
. Then there is a unique solution
f_{j}
on
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Moreover, for
a\leq r-\frac{s}{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}d{f}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq{M}_{3}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq{M}_{3}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}),\end{split}

(8.11)

where
{M}_{3}
is a constant depends on
C_{0},c_{0},M_{*},s
.

Based on Proposition
8.1
, we are now ready to prove Theorem
1.3
.

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.

For there are four points in Theorem
1.3
, so we divide the proof into four steps, The following Step 1 and Step 2 is for Point 1 and Point 2 in Theorem
1.3
. Step 3 is for giving a proof to Point 3. At last, we prove Point 4 in Step 4.

Step 1: Obtaining a solution of Theorem
1.3
in a weaker spaces by taking a limit.

If we set

\mathbf{U}_{j}=(p(h_{j}),u^{1}_{j},u^{2}_{j},u^{3}_{j})^{\mathrm{T}},

by Lemma
2.14
and (
2.17
), for
j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
we have

\begin{split}&A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{j}+A^{a}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{a}\mathbf{U}_{j}=0,\\
&\partial_{t}w_{j}^{\alpha}+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{a}\partial_{a}w_{j}^{\alpha}+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{\alpha}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h_{j}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}^{\alpha}+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}w_{j}^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}^{\kappa}=0.\end{split}

Then for any
j,l\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})+A^{a}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{a}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})=

\displaystyle-\{A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{j})-A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{l})\}\partial_{\alpha}\mathbf{U}_{l},

(8.12)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(w_{j}^{\alpha}-w_{l}^{\alpha})+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{a}\partial_{a}(w_{j}^{\alpha}-w_{l}^{\alpha})=

\displaystyle\{(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{a}-(u_{l}^{0})^{-1}u_{l}^{a}\}\partial_{a}w_{l}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{\alpha}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h_{j}+(u_{l}^{0})^{-1}u_{l}^{\alpha}w_{l}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h_{l}

(8.13)

\displaystyle+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}^{\alpha}-(u_{l}^{0})^{-1}w_{l}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}u_{l}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}w_{j}^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j}^{\kappa}+(u_{l}^{0})^{-1}w_{l}^{\alpha}\partial_{\kappa}u_{l}^{\kappa}.

Using the estimates (
8.6
)-(
8.9
) in Proposition
8.1
, we can show that

\small\|\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}\leq C_{{M}_{2}}(\|\mathbf{u}_{0j}-\mathbf{u}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|h_{0j}-h_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

Here
C_{{M}_{2}}
is a constant depending on
{M}_{2}
. By Lemma
2.3
, so we get

\small\|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}-\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{l},h_{j}-h_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}\leq C_{{M}_{2}}(\|\mathbf{u}_{0j}-\mathbf{u}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|h_{0j}-h_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

For
u^{0}_{j}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}|^{2}}
, so we can bound

\small\|{\mathbf{u}}_{j}-{\mathbf{u}}_{l},h_{j}-h_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}\leq C_{{M}_{2}}(\|\mathbf{u}_{0j}-\mathbf{u}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|h_{0j}-h_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

Using (
8.12
) and (
8.13
) again, we have

\small\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{2}}(\|\mathbf{v}_{0j}-\mathbf{v}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0j}-\rho_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

By Lemma
2.3
and
u^{0}_{j}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}|^{2}}
, it follows

\small\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{u}_{j}-\mathbf{u}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{2}}(\|\mathbf{u}_{0j}-\mathbf{u}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|h_{0j}-h_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

Therefore, the sequence
\{(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
H^{1}_{x}
, and
\{(\partial_{t}h_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
L^{2}_{x}
. So there is a limit
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})
such that

\begin{split}(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\rightarrow&(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})\quad\quad\ \ \quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{1}\times H_{x}^{1}\times H_{x}^{1},\\
(\partial_{t}h_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\rightarrow&(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})\quad\text{in}\ \ L_{x}^{2}\times L_{x}^{2}\times L_{x}^{2}.\end{split}

(8.14)

By using (
8.6
) and (
8.7
), then a subsequence of
\{(\mathbf{u}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
and
\{(\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j},\partial_{t}\rho_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
are weakly convergent. Therefore, if
m\rightarrow\infty
, then

\begin{split}(h_{j_{m}},\mathbf{u}_{j_{m}},\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}})\rightharpoonup&(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})\quad\qquad\ \ \text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{2},\\
(\partial_{t}h_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}})\rightharpoonup&(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{1}.\end{split}

(8.15)

By (
8.14
), (
8.15
) and interpolation formula, we can obtain

(h_{j_{m}},\mathbf{u}_{j_{m}})\rightarrow(h,\mathbf{u})\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s_{1}}(0\leq s_{1}<s),\quad\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}}\rightarrow\mathbf{w}\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s_{2}}(0\leq s_{2}<2).

(8.16)

By (
8.16
), (
8.12
), and (
8.13
), we also have

\begin{split}&(\partial_{t}h_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j_{m}})\rightarrow(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u})\ \qquad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s_{3}}(0\leq s_{3}<s-1),\\
&\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}}\rightarrow\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s_{4}}(0\leq s_{4}<1).\end{split}

(8.17)

The weak convergence (
8.15
) and the strong convergence (
8.16
)-(
8.17
) makes us easy to check that
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})
is a strong solution of (
1.20
) with the initial data
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})|_{t=0}=(h_{0},\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
,
and for
1\leq s_{1}<s,1\leq s_{2}<2
,

\begin{split}&(h,u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s_{1}}),\qquad\qquad\quad\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s_{2}}),\\
&(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u})\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s_{1}-1}),\qquad\qquad\ \ \partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s_{2}-1}),\\
&(h,u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s}),\qquad\qquad\ \ \mathbf{w}\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{2}),\\
&(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u})\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s-1}),\ \quad\qquad\quad\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{1}).\end{split}

(8.18)

Furthermore, by using (
8.6
) and (
8.7
), we obtain

\begin{split}&{E}(t)=\|(h,u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\leq{M}_{2},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}&\|dh\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}+\|d\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\leq{M}_{2},\\
&\|\partial_{t}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{1}}\leq{M}_{2}.\end{split}

It also remains for us that the convergence (
8.16
) and (
8.17
) also hold in the highest derivatives.

Step 2: Strong convergence of the limit in highest derivatives.
Denote

\begin{split}e^{(\alpha)}_{j}=&\|u^{\alpha}_{0(j+1)}-u^{\alpha}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}},\quad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
d^{(\alpha)}_{j}=&\|w^{\alpha}_{0(j+1)}-w^{\alpha}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}},\quad\alpha=0,1,2,3,\\
e^{(4)}_{j}=&\|h_{0(j+1)}-h_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}.\end{split}

(8.19)

Set

\begin{split}e_{j}=\textstyle{\sum}_{\alpha=0}^{3}(e^{(\alpha)}_{j}+d^{(\alpha)}_{j})+e^{(4)}_{j}.\end{split}

(8.20)

Therefore, by using (
8.1
), (
8.2
), (
8.19
) and (
8.20
), it yields

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}e_{j}=0.\end{split}

(8.21)

Using (
8.1
) and (
8.2
) again, we can obtain:

(i) uniform bounds

\begin{split}&\|(h_{0j},u^{0}_{0j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j})\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim M_{*}+M^{5}_{*},\quad\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim M_{*}+M^{5}_{*},\end{split}

(8.22)

(ii) high frequency bounds

\begin{split}&\|(h_{0j},u^{0}_{0j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j})\|_{H^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{3}}\lesssim 2^{j}(M_{*}+M^{5}_{*}),\end{split}

(8.23)

(iii) difference bounds

\begin{split}&\|h_{0(j+1)}-h_{0j},\mathbf{u}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{u}_{0j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq 2^{-sj}e_{j},\\
&\|\mathbf{w}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq 2^{-2j}e_{j},\end{split}

(8.24)

(iv) limit

\lim_{l\rightarrow\infty}(h_{0j},\mathbf{u}_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j})=(h_{0},\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\quad\mathrm{in}\quad H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{2}.

(8.25)

For the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
, we claim that

\bullet
uniform bounds

\|h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|(u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\lesssim{M}_{2},

(8.26)

\bullet
higher-order norms

\|h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|(u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{3}}\lesssim 2^{j}{M}_{2},

(8.27)

\bullet
energy bounds of the difference

\|(h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j},

(8.28)

and

\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(s-1)j}e_{j},

(8.29)

and

\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}\dot{H}_{x}^{2}}\lesssim e_{j},

(8.30)

\bullet
Strichartz estimates of the difference

2^{j}\|h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\lesssim e_{j}.

(8.31)

By using (
8.14
), (
8.28
), (
8.29
) and (
8.30
), we get

\|\mathbf{u}_{j}-\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}.

Hence, combining (
8.19
) and (
8.20
), we have

\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{u}_{j}-\mathbf{u}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0.

(8.32)

Similarly, using (
8.28
), (
8.29
) and (
8.30
), so we also conclude that

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|h_{j}-h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0,\\
\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{2}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0.\end{split}

(8.33)

Combining (
8.32
) and (
8.33
), we have proved the strong convergence
\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})=(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})
in
H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{2}
. In a similar way, we can show that
\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}(\partial_{t}h_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{u}_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})=(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})
in
H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{1}
. Therefore, we conclude that
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})
is a strong solution of (
1.20
) with the initial data
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w})|_{t=0}=(h_{0},\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
. Moreover, we have

\begin{split}&(h,u^{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}})\in C([0,T^{*}];H^{s}),\quad\quad\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H^{2}),\\
&(\partial_{t}h,\partial_{t}\mathbf{u})\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s-1}),\qquad\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{1}).\end{split}

(8.34)

It now remains for us to prove (
8.26
)-(
8.31
). By using (
8.6
), we get (
8.26
). By Corollary
3.4
, (
8.6
), (
8.9
) and (
8.23
), we can obtain (
8.27
).

The proof of (
8.28
).
By (
8.12
), we can see

\begin{split}A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{j+1})\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{j+1}-\mathbf{U}_{j})+A^{a}(\mathbf{U}_{j+1})\partial_{a}(\mathbf{U}_{j+1}-\mathbf{U}_{j})=&-\{A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{j+1})-A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\}\partial_{\alpha}\mathbf{U}_{j}.\end{split}

Multiplying with
\mathbf{U}_{j+1}-\mathbf{U}_{j}
, integrating it on
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, and using (
8.9
) and (
8.24
), it follows

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{U}_{j+1}-\mathbf{U}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|(\mathbf{U}_{j+1}-\mathbf{U}_{j})(0,\cdot)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\exp\left(\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|d\mathbf{U}_{j},d\mathbf{U}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right)\lesssim(2^{-sj}e_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

Therefore, by Lemma
2.3
, we can obtain

\begin{split}\|(h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j+1}-\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j}.\end{split}

For
u^{0}_{j}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}|^{2}}
, we can also get
\|{u}^{0}_{j+1}-{u}^{0}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j}
. To summarize the outcome, we have proved

\begin{split}\|(h_{j+1}-h_{j},{\mathbf{u}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j}.\end{split}

(8.35)

It implies that we have proved (
8.28
).

The proof of (
8.29
).
By using (
8.13
), we get

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})+((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j+1}\cdot\nabla)(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\\
=&\left\{\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j+1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\right)\cdot\nabla\right\}\mathbf{w}_{j}\\
&-\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}_{j+1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\right)w_{j+1}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}h_{j+1}\\
&-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}_{j}(w_{j+1}^{\kappa}-w_{j}^{\kappa})\partial_{\kappa}h_{j+1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}{\mathbf{u}}_{j}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\\
&+\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)w_{j+1}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\mathbf{u}_{j+1}+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}(w_{j+1}^{\kappa}-w_{j}^{\kappa})\partial_{\kappa}\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\\
&+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}w_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})-\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\mathbf{w}_{j+1}\partial_{\kappa}u_{j+1}^{\kappa}\\
&-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\partial_{\kappa}u_{j+1}^{\kappa}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\mathbf{w}_{j}\partial_{\kappa}(u_{j+1}^{\kappa}-u_{j}^{\kappa})\end{split}

(8.36)

Multiplying (
8.36
) with
\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}
and integrating it on
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{w}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh_{j+1},d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|dh_{j+1},d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d(h_{j+1}-h_{j}),d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.37)

By Hölder’s inequality, we can bound (
8.37
) by

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{w}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh_{j+1},d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(\|\nabla\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|dh_{j+1},d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}})d\tau\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla(h_{j+1}-h_{j}),\nabla(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.38)

Due to (
8.6
), (
8.7
), (
8.8
), (
8.9
), (
8.24
), and (
8.28
), it yields

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq&C(2^{-sj}e_{j})^{2}+CM^{2}_{2}(2^{-sj}e_{j})^{2}2^{-\frac{j}{2}}+CM^{2}_{2}(2^{-(s-1)j}e_{j})^{2}\\
&+C\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|dh_{j+1},d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.39)

Using Gronwall’s inequality for (
8.39
), then (
8.29
) holds.

It remains for us to prove (
8.30
)-(
8.31
). By bootstrap arguments, if we assume

\begin{split}2^{j}\|(h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\lesssim&2^{-\delta_{1}j}e_{j},\\
\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}\dot{H}_{x}^{2}}\leq&2C_{0}e_{j},\end{split}

(8.40)

then we obtain

2^{j}\|(h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2\delta_{1}j}e_{j},

(8.41)

and

\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}\dot{H}_{x}^{2}}\leq C_{0}e_{j}.

(8.42)

Hence, (
8.30
) and (
8.41
) holds. Above,
C_{0}=3C^{2}+C
, and
C
is a universal constant which is defined in (
8.62
) below. So our goal now is to prove (
8.41
) and (
8.42
).

The proof of (
8.42
).
Set
\mathbf{W}_{j}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{h_{j}}\mathbf{w}_{j})
and
\mathbf{G}_{j}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathbf{W}_{j})
. From (
3.87
), (
3.88
) and (
2.14
), we get

\begin{split}\|\Delta(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq&C\|\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathrm{vort}\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}+\|(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\cdot(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}\\
&+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}\\
&+\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\cdot\nabla\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+\|d\mathbf{u}_{j}\cdot\nabla(\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j+1}-\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j+1})\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{W}_{j+1}-\mathbf{W}_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}+C\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j+1}-\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|(\nabla\mathbf{u}_{j},\nabla h_{j})\|_{{H}_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&+C\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|\nabla(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),\nabla(h_{j+1}-h_{j}))\|_{{H}_{x}^{1}}.\end{split}

By using (
8.28
), (
8.6
), (
8.29
), and
\|f\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\|f\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L_{x}^{2}}\|f\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{{H}_{x}^{2}}
, we can prove that

\begin{split}\|\Delta(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{W}_{j+1}-\mathbf{W}_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-(s-2)j}e_{j}.\end{split}

(8.43)

Similarly, seeing from (
3.97
), (
3.99
), and (
2.14
), we have

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{W}_{j+1}-\mathbf{W}_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{1}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+C\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j+1}-\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{{H}_{x}^{s-1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{{H}_{x}^{s-1}})\\
&+C\|\mathring{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{{H}_{x}^{1}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{{H}_{x}^{s-1}})\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-(s-2)j}e_{j}.\end{split}

(8.44)

In view of (
8.43
) and (
8.44
), for large
j
, we have

\begin{split}\|\Delta(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+CM_{2}C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-(s-2)j}e_{j}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}+Ce_{j}.\end{split}

(8.45)

Now, we need to estimate
\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}
. By (
3.28
), we can directly have

\begin{split}(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\kappa}\left(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha}\right)=&(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}E_{j}^{\alpha}+\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\Gamma_{j}\right)-\Gamma_{j}\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right),\end{split}

where
F_{j}^{\alpha}
and
E_{j}^{\alpha}
is the
\alpha
-th component of
\mathbf{F}_{j}
and
\mathbf{E}_{j}
, and
\mathbf{F}_{j}
,
\mathbf{E}_{j}
is defined in the same way as above, but with
h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w},\mathbf{W}
replaced by
h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j},\mathbf{W}_{j}
. As a result, the difference term
\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}
satisfies

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}\left\{(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right\}+(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}u_{j+1}^{a}\partial_{a}\left\{(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right\}\\
=&\left\{(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}u_{j+1}^{a}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{a}\right\}\partial_{a}(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})+\left\{(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right\}E_{j+1}^{\alpha}\\
&+(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}(E_{j+1}^{\alpha}-E_{j}^{\alpha})-(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j})\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}\right)-\Gamma_{j}\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\\
&+\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\Gamma_{j+1}\right\}+\partial^{\alpha}\left\{(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\left(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\right)\right\}.\end{split}

(8.46)

Multiplying with
(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})
on (
8.46
) and integrating it on
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, so we get

\begin{split}&\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\|(\mathbf{G}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{G}_{0j})-(\mathbf{F}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{F}_{0j})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
=&\underbrace{-\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}u_{j+1}^{a}\partial_{a}\left\{(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right\}(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{1}}\\
&+\underbrace{\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}u_{j+1}^{a}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}u_{j}^{a}\right\}\partial_{a}(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{2}}\\
&+\underbrace{\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{(u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right\}E_{j+1}^{\alpha}(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{3}}\\
&+\underbrace{\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}(E_{j+1}^{\alpha}-E_{j}^{\alpha})(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{4}}\\
&\underbrace{-\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j})\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}\right)(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{5}}\\
&\underbrace{-\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma_{j}\partial^{\alpha}\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)(G_{j}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{6}}\\
&+\underbrace{\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}\left\{\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\Gamma_{j+1}\right\}\left((G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right)dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{7}}\\
&+\underbrace{\textstyle{\int}^{T^{*}}_{0}\textstyle{\int}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}\left\{(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\left(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\right)\right\}\left((G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right)dxd\tau}_{\equiv J_{8}}.\end{split}

(8.47)

Bound of the right hand of (
8.47
)
. We now turn to estimate
\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}
,
\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}
,
\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}
and
\mathbf{E}_{j+1}-\mathbf{E}_{j}
. Recall that
\mathbf{G}_{j}
has the same formulations with
\mathbf{G}
only by replacing
(h,\mathbf{u},\mathbf{w},\mathbf{W})
to
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j},\mathbf{W}_{j})
. Applying (
1.18
) and (
2.30
), we can obtain

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(1+\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}})\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|(d\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-d\mathbf{w}_{j})\cdot(du_{j},dh_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot(du_{j+1}-du_{j},dh_{j+1}-dh_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\cdot dh_{j}\cdot dh_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot(dh_{j+1}-dh_{j})\cdot dh_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

Due to Hölder’s inequality, (
8.6
), (
8.28
), (
8.29
), and (
8.40
), we can show that

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&2C(1+M_{2}^{2})(1+C_{0})e_{j}.\end{split}

(8.48)

By Hölder’s inequality, (
8.6
), (
8.28
), and (
8.29
), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})\big(\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\big)\\
\leq&C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})\big(\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\big)\\
\leq&2C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j}).\end{split}

(8.49)

In a similar way, we can also bound
\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}
by

\begin{split}\|\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&C\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-d\mathbf{u}_{j})\cdot d\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{u}_{j}\cdot(d\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-d\mathbf{w}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&+C\|(\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j})\cdot dh_{j}\cdot\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot(dh_{j+1}-dh_{j})\cdot\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})\big(\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\big)\\
\leq&2C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j}).\end{split}

(8.50)

Using Hölder’s inequality, we can estimate
\mathbf{E}_{j+1}-\mathbf{E}_{j}
by

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{E}_{j+1}-\mathbf{E}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}})\\
&+C\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}})\\
&+C\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}})\\
&+C\|d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j}\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

Applying (
8.6
), (
8.28
), and (
8.29
), it follows

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{E}_{j+1}-\mathbf{E}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})\|d(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}),d(h_{j+1}-h_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+C(1+M_{2})\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\\
&+CM_{2}\|(d\mathbf{u}_{j},dh_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\\
&+CM_{2}(\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j},h_{j+1}-h_{j}\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\\
&+C(M^{2}_{2}+M^{3}_{2})\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.51)

Now, we are ready to bound the terms
J_{1},J_{2},\cdots
, and
J_{6}
. Integrating
J_{1}
by parts, it’s direct for us to obtain

\begin{split}|J_{1}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

Let
T^{*}\leq 1
. When
j
is large enough, applying (
8.49
), (
8.48
) and (
8.9
), we get

\begin{split}|J_{1}|\leq&CM_{2}(T^{*})^{\frac{1}{2}}\{2C(1+M_{2}^{2})(1+C_{0})e_{j}\}^{2}\\
&+\{2C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j})\}^{2}CM_{2}(T^{*})^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.52)

For
j
is large enough, by using Hölder’s inequality, (
8.48
), and (
8.40
) we can get

\begin{split}|J_{2}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla(\mathbf{G}_{j}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}2^{j}\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{G}_{j}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau\\
\leq&C(M_{2}+M^{3}_{2})\int^{T^{*}}_{0}2^{j}\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}\int^{T^{*}}_{0}2^{j}\|(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.53)

Similarly, we can also obtain

\begin{split}|J_{3}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{E}_{j+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.54)

\begin{split}|J_{4}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{E}_{j+1}-\mathbf{E}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.55)

\begin{split}|J_{5}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{u}_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.56)

\begin{split}|J_{6}|\leq&C\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\Gamma_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|d\left(\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\right)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.57)

Let us consider
J_{7}
and
J_{8}
, for it’s different and difficult. Integrating
J_{7}
by parts, we get

\begin{split}J_{7}=&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\Gamma_{j+1}\left((G_{j+1}^{0}-G_{j}^{0})-(F_{j+1}^{0}-F_{j}^{0})\right)dx|^{T^{*}}_{0}\\
&-\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{\left((u_{j+1}^{0})^{-1}-(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\right)\Gamma_{j+1}\right\}\partial_{\alpha}\left((G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right)dxd\tau.\end{split}

Applying Hölder’s inequality, and Bernstein’s inequality, we can obtain

\begin{split}J_{7}\leq&C\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}})\\
&+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{\alpha}(G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{\alpha}(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}})\\
&+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}2^{j}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

Due to (
8.28
), (
8.40
), (
8.29
), (
8.6
), (
8.48
), and (
8.49
), for large enough

, it follows

\begin{split}|J_{7}|\leq&C\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}})\\
&+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}2^{j}\|\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Gamma_{j+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau\\
\leq&C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})2^{-\frac{j}{2}}e_{j}\left\{C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j})+2C(1+M_{2}^{2})(1+C_{0})e_{j}\right\}\\
&+C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})2^{-\delta_{1}j}e_{j}\left\{C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j})+2C(1+M_{2}^{2})(1+C_{0})e_{j}\right\}\\
\leq&(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.58)

For
J_{8}
, we also have

\begin{split}J_{8}=&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\left(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\right)\left((G_{j+1}^{0}-G_{j}^{0})-(F_{j+1}^{0}-F_{j}^{0})\right)dx|^{T^{*}}_{0}\\
&-\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u_{j}^{0})^{-1}\left(\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\right)\partial_{\alpha}\left((G_{j+1}^{\alpha}-G_{j}^{\alpha})-(F_{j+1}^{\alpha}-F_{j}^{\alpha})\right)dxd\tau\\
\leq&C\|\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}})\\
&+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}\|\Gamma_{j+1}-\Gamma_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

Similarly, by using (
8.28
), (
8.40
), (

), and (
8.6
), we also obtain

\begin{split}J_{8}\leq(Ce_{j})^{2}.\end{split}

(8.59)

Bound of the left hand of (
8.47
)
. By (
8.24
) and (
8.49
), we deduce

\begin{split}&\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\|(\mathbf{G}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{G}_{0j})-(\mathbf{F}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{F}_{0j})\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\geq&\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\left(2C(M_{2}+M_{2}^{2})(C_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\frac{(s-1)j}{4}}e_{j}+2^{-(s-\frac{3}{2})j}e_{j})\right)^{2}-(Ce_{j})^{2}\\
\geq&\|\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-(Ce_{j})^{2},\end{split}

(8.60)

for
j
is large enough. Combining (

), (
8.52
)-(
8.60
), we get

\begin{split}&\|(\mathbf{G}_{j+1}-\mathbf{G}_{j})-(\mathbf{F}_{j+1}-\mathbf{F}_{j})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq 3Ce_{j}.\end{split}

(8.61)

Since (

) and (
8.61
), we have proved

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}\leq(3C^{2}+C)e_{j}.\end{split}

(8.62)

Therefore, we have proved (
8.42
).

The proof of (
8.41
).
By using (
8.10
)-(
8.11
) and taking
r=\frac{s}{2}=1+5\delta_{1}
, we can bound (
8.41
) by

\begin{split}&2^{j}\|h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\\
\leq&C2^{j}(\|h_{j+1}-h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j+1}-\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{1+5\delta_{1}}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+5\delta_{1}}})\\
\leq&C2^{-3\delta_{1}j}e_{j}.\end{split}

Then (
8.41
) holds. This implies that (
8.31
) holds.

Step 3: Obtaining a solution of linear wave by taking a limit.

Reapting the proof in Step 1 and Step 2, we shall also find

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|f_{j}-f\|_{H^{r}_{x}}=0,\quad\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\partial_{t}f_{j}-\partial_{t}f\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}=0.\end{split}

(8.63)

So taking
j\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\begin{cases}&\square_{g}f=0,\qquad(t,x)\in(t_{0},T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
&(f,\partial_{t}f)|_{t=0}=(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H_{x}^{r-1}.\end{cases}

Using (
8.11
), it follows

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}d{f}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(8.64)

Step 4: Continuous dependence.

Based on (
8.64
), we can prove the continuous dependence of solutions on the initial data, which can follow Section
7
. Therefore, we finish the proof of Theorem
1.3
.
∎

8.3.
Proof of Proposition
8.1
by Proposition
8.2

In this part, we prove Proposition
8.1
through Proposition
8.2
. We observe that the corresponding statement In Proposition
8.2
considers the small, supported data. While, the initial data is large in Proposition
8.1
, and regularity of the vorticity in Proposition
8.1
is weaker than Proposition
8.2
. So we divide the proof into three subparts. Firstly, we can only get a solution of Proposition
8.1
on a short time interval, which is presented in subsection
8.3.1
. Then we are able to get some good Strichartz estimates for low-frequency and mid-frequency of solutions on these short time intervals by paying a loss of derivatives, which is proved in subsection
8.3.2
. After that, we can extend these solutions on a regular time-interval in subsection
8.3.3
. Moreover, the solutions of linear wave can also be extended to the regular time-interval, which is obtained in
8.3.4
.

8.3.1.
Energy estimates and Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

Firstly, by using a scaling method, we can reduce the initial data in Proposition
8.1
to be small. Considering (
8.1
), for
T>0
, take space-time scaling

\begin{split}&\underline{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j}=\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}(Tt,Tx),\quad\underline{h}_{0j}=h_{0j}(Tt,Tx).\end{split}

Referring to (
1.1
) and (
1.15
), we set

\begin{split}&\underline{u}^{0}_{0j}=\sqrt{1+|\underline{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j}|^{2}},\qquad\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j}=(\underline{u}^{0}_{0j},\underline{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})^{\mathrm{T}},\end{split}

and

\underline{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{\underline{h}_{0}}\underline{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}).

then we have the bounds

\begin{split}\|(\underline{u}^{0}_{0j}-1,\underline{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\underline{h}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}\leq&T^{s-\frac{3}{2}}(\|({u}^{0}_{0j}-1,{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|{h}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}),\\
\|\underline{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}\leq&T^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}\|\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{{h}_{0}}{\mathbf{u}}_{0j})\|_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}\\
=&T^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}(1+\|h_{0j}\|^{4}_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}+\|{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}\|^{4}_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}})\\
\leq&T^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}2^{\delta_{1}j}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{{H}^{2}}(1+\|h_{0j}\|^{4}_{{H}^{s}}+\|(u^{0}_{0}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j})\|^{4}_{{H}^{s}}).\end{split}

\begin{split}\widetilde{Q}_{j}^{\alpha}=&\widetilde{Q}_{1\kappa j}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{h}_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\widetilde{u}_{j}^{\kappa}+\widetilde{Q}_{2\kappa\delta j}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{u}_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}u_{j}^{\delta}+\widetilde{Q}_{3j}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{h}_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\widetilde{h}_{j},\\
\widetilde{D}_{j}^{\alpha}=&\widetilde{D}_{1\kappa j}^{\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{h}_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\widetilde{u}_{j}^{\kappa}+\widetilde{D}_{2\kappa\delta j}^{\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{u}_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{\gamma}\widetilde{u}_{j}^{\delta}+\widetilde{D}_{3j}^{\beta\gamma}\partial_{\beta}\widetilde{h}_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\widetilde{h}_{j},\end{split}

and the functions

by replacing

\|(\widetilde{u}^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{h}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{2+\delta_{1}}}\leq\epsilon_{2},

(8.82)

and

\|d\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}_{j},d\widetilde{h}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[-2,2]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq\epsilon_{2}.

&(f,\partial_{t}f)|_{t=t_{0}}=(f_{0},f_{1}),\end{cases}

(8.84)

admits a solution

For all

Note (

Therefore, if we set

\begin{split}&\bar{h}_{j}(t,x)=\textstyle{\sum}_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\psi(x-y)(\widetilde{h}_{j}+\underline{h}_{0j}(y)),\\
&\mathring{\bar{\mathbf{u}}}_{j}(t,x)=\textstyle{\sum}_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\psi(x-y)(\mathring{\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}}_{j}+\mathring{\underline{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j}(y)),\\
&\bar{u}^{0}_{j}=\sqrt{1+|\mathring{\bar{\mathbf{u}}}_{j}|^{2}},\quad\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j}=(\bar{u}^{0}_{j},\mathring{\bar{\mathbf{u}}}_{j})^{\mathrm{T}},\quad\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{j}(t,x)=\mathrm{vort}(\mathrm{e}^{\bar{h}_{j}}\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j}).\end{split}

(8.87)

By (

\displaystyle(h,{\mathbf{u}}_{j},{\mathbf{w}}_{j})=(\bar{h}_{j},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j},\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{j})((T^{*}_{j})^{-1}t,(T^{*}_{j})^{-1}x),

(8.88)

\displaystyle(h,{\mathbf{u}}_{j},{\mathbf{w}}_{j})|_{t=0}=(\bar{h}_{j},\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j},\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{j})(0,(T^{*}_{j})^{-1}x)=(h_{0j},\mathbf{u}_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j}).

(8.89)

Thus,

is a smooth solution of (

\begin{split}\|d\bar{h}_{j},d\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,1]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&\sup_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|d\widetilde{h}_{j},d\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,1]}{C^{\delta}_{x}}}\\
\leq&C(\|(\widetilde{u}^{0}_{0j}-1,\mathring{\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{h}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{2}}).\end{split}

(8.90)

By changing of coordinates

, for each

, we therefore get

\begin{split}\|d{h}_{j},d{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{1}{2}+{\delta})}\|d\bar{h}_{j},d\bar{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,1]}C^{\delta}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.91)

Using (

), (

), and (

), and
{\delta}\in(0,s-2)
, it follows

\begin{split}\|d{h}_{j},d{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&C(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{1}{2}+{\delta})}(\|(\widetilde{u}^{0}_{0j}-1,\mathring{\widetilde{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{h}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{2}})\\
\leq&C(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{1}{2}+{\delta})}\{(T^{*}_{j})^{s-\frac{3}{2}}\|(h,{u}^{0}_{0j}-1,{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s}}+(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{3}{2}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{2}}\}\\
\leq&C(\|(h_{0},{u}^{0}_{0j}-1,{\mathring{\mathbf{u}}}_{0j})\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{2}}).\end{split}

(8.92)

Comining (
8.92
) and (
8.4
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|d{h}_{j},d{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E(0)).\end{split}

(8.93)

Set

E(T^{*}_{j})=\|h_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{s}_{x}}+\|(u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{2}_{x}}.

(8.94)

By using (
8.93
) and Theorem
3.3
, we have
35
35

35

Note that
\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H_{x}^{2+}}
is not uniform bounded. But
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{w}}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{2+}}\lesssim\epsilon_{2}
for all

.

\begin{split}E(T^{*}_{j})\leq&C(E(0)+E^{5}(0))\exp\left(5(1+E(0))\mathrm{e}^{5(1+E(0))}\right).\end{split}

(8.95)

We also expect some estimates for the homogeneous linear wave equation on a short time interval
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. Following the proof of (
4.60
) and (
4.63
) for Equation (
4.54
), we deduce that for
1\leq r_{1}\leq s+1
, then

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{j}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f,f_{t})|_{t=0}=(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r_{1}}\times H^{r_{1}-1}_{x},\end{cases}

(8.96)

has a unique solution on
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. Moreover, for
a_{1}<r_{1}-1
, we have

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a_{1}-1}df\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r_{1}}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r_{1}-1}}),\end{split}

(8.97)

and

\begin{split}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{r_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{j}]}H^{r_{1}-1}_{x}}\leq C(\|f_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r_{1}}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r_{1}-1}}).\end{split}

(8.98)

Seeing from (
8.97
) and (
8.98
), there is a uniform bound for
h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j}
and
\mathbf{w}_{j}
. Unfortunately, the time interval
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
is not uniform for

. If the sequence
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
can be applied in the analysis, then we have to extend this solution on a regular time interval. In fact, (
8.93
) is a strong Strichartz estimate given the derivatives. If we only obtain a loss of derivative for Strichartz estimates, the time interval could be regular.

8.3.2.
A loss of Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

To get a loss of Strichartz estimates on a short-time-interval, we will discuss it by using phase truncation
36
36

36

Here, we mainly inspired by Tataru
[
51
]
, Bahouri-Chemin
[
11
]
, Ai-Ifrim-Tataru
[
2
]
, and Andersson-Zhang
[
8
]
. Similar to
[
8
]
, we conclude the proof by induction method.
. Next, we divide it into two cases: the high and low frequency for
(h_{j},{\mathbf{u}}_{j})
.

Case 1: High frequency(
k\geq j
).
Using (
8.93
), we get

\begin{split}&\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\leq C(1+E(0)),\quad a\in[0,s-2).\end{split}

(8.99)

For
k\geq j
, by Bernstein inequality and (
8.99
), we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&2^{-ka}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}(1+E(0)).\end{split}

(8.100)

Taking
a=9\delta_{1}
in (
8.100
), so we obtain

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(8.101)

Case 2: Low frequency(
k<j
).
In this case, it’s a little different from
k\geq j
. Fortunately, there is some good estimates for difference terms
P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{u}}_{m})
. Following (
1.23
) and (
1.24
), we set

\begin{split}&u_{j}^{\alpha}=u^{\alpha}_{j+}+u^{\alpha}_{j-},\quad{\mathbf{P}}_{j}=-{\mathrm{P}}_{j}^{\beta\gamma}\partial^{2}_{\beta\gamma}\\
&\mathbf{P}_{j}u_{j-}^{\alpha}=\mathrm{e}^{-h_{j}}W_{j}^{\alpha},\quad\mathrm{P}_{j}^{\beta\gamma}=m^{\beta\gamma}+2u_{j}^{\beta}u_{j}^{\gamma}.\end{split}

(8.102)

We will obtain some good estimates of
P_{k}(d{h}_{m+1}-d{h}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{u}}_{m})
by using a Strichartz estimates (
8.97
) of (
8.96
). The good estimate is from a loss of derivatives of Strichartz estimates.

For
m\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, by Bernstein inequality, we get

\begin{split}&\|{\mathbf{u}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sm}\|{\mathbf{u}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}},\\
&\|{h}_{0(m+1)}-{h}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sm}\|h_{0(m+1)}-h_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}},\\
&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2m}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{2}}.\end{split}

(8.103)

We claim that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{u}_{m+1}-\mathbf{u}_{m},h_{m+1}-h_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-sm}E(0),

(8.104)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}_{m+1}-\mathbf{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle C2^{-(s-1)m}E(0),

(8.105)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}_{m+1}-\mathbf{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle CE(0).

(8.106)

We remark that the proof of (
8.104
)-(
8.106
) can repeat the proof of (
8.28
)-(
8.30
). So we omit the details.

On the other hand, using Strichartz estimates (
8.97
) and interpolation formula, then

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({h}_{m+1}-{h}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{u}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{+m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|h_{m+1}-h_{m},{\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{2+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{2}+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|h_{m+1}-h_{m},{\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{2+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
&+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{\frac{1}{4}-\delta_{1}}_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{\frac{3}{4}+\delta_{1}}_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.107)

Noting
s=2+40\delta_{1}
and using (
8.104
)-(
8.106
), we can bound (
8.107
) by

\begin{split}&\|\nabla(h_{m+1}-h_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{u}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{+m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq 2^{-7\delta_{1}m}E(0).\end{split}

(8.108)

By (
8.105
) and Sobolev imbeddings, we shall get

\begin{split}\|\nabla({\mathbf{u}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{-m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|\nabla({\mathbf{u}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{u}}_{-m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{2}(0))2^{-8\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.109)

If we add (
8.108
) and (
8.109
), then we have

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{2}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.110)

By using (
2.14
) and (
8.110
), we shall obtain

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}(h_{m+1}-h_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\nabla(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),\nabla({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\cdot(1+\|(h_{m},u^{0}_{m}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{s}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.111)

Due to (
8.110
) and (
8.111
), for
k<j
, it yields

\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m},

(8.112)

and

\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(8.113)

Based on (
8.101
), (
8.112
), (
8.113
), following section 10.3.3 in
[
8
]
, we can extend the solution
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
to a uniformly regular time-interval.

8.3.3.
Uniform energy and Strichartz estimates on a regular time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

We recall
T_{N_{0}}^{*}=[E(0)]^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}
, which is stated in (
8.70
). Note (
8.101
) and (
8.112
). Therefore, when
k\geq j
and
j\geq N_{0}
, using (
8.101
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.114)

When
k<j
and
m\geq N_{0}
, using (
8.112
) and Hölder’s inequality, we also see

\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(8.115)

On the other side, when
k\geq j
and
j<N_{0}
, using (
8.101
), we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}dh_{j},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.116)

When
k<j
and
m<N_{0}
, using (
8.112
) and Hölder’s inequality, we can see

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d(h_{m+1}-h_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{u}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{u}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.117)

Due to a different time-interval for the sequency
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
, so we discuss the solutions
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
or
j\geq N_{0}+1
as follows.

Case 1:
j\leq N_{0}
.
In this case,
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
exists on
[0,T_{j}^{*}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. So we don’t need to extend the solution
(h_{j},\mathbf{u}_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
. Using (
8.93
) and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+E(0)).

By (
8.73
), this yields

\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2.

(8.118)

By the Newton-Leibniz formula and (

\|h_{j},u^{0}_{j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq|h_{0j},u^{0}_{0j}-1,\mathring{\mathbf{u}}_{0j}|+\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0}.

(8.119)

Using (
8.118
) and Theorem
3.3
, we get

E(T^{*}_{N_{0}})\leq C(E(0)+E^{5}(0))\exp(5\textrm{e}^{5})=C_{*}.

(8.120)

Here

is stated as in (
8.74
).

Case 2:
j\geq N_{0}+1
.

To be simple, we set

I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]=[t_{0},t_{1}],\quad\quad|I_{1}|=[E(0)]^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

(8.121)

By a frequency decomposition, we get

\begin{split}d\mathbf{u}_{j}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}d\mathbf{u}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\\
=&P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{u}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\mathbf{u}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{u}_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{k}.\end{split}

(8.122)

Similarly, we also have

\begin{split}dh_{j}=&P_{\geq j}dh_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}dh_{k}.\end{split}

(8.123)

When
k\geq j
, using (
8.114
) and (
8.116
), we have
37

Step 1: Extending

To start, referring (

\begin{split}\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}dh_{j},P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}dh_{k},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m}),P_{k}(d\mathbf{u}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{u}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}dh_{j},P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}dh_{k},P_{k}d\mathbf{u}_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(dh_{m+1}-dh_{m}),P_{k}(d\mathbf{u}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{u}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

So we get

\begin{split}\|dh_{j},d\mathbf{u}_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}
